Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : HAK6_2014 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : CEKCBR
Hangi Tablo için : CKSN22
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Çek Çýkýþ Bordrosu
Kod : 
Public ustmargin, solmargin, sagMargin, satirYuksekligi, sirano, MainTableE, MainTableT		
Dim sayfaSatirsayisi, AraToplam, Grid, satirSonu, satirBaslangic
Dim solTutar, GenTutar, solPB, GenPB, xCizgi(), sayfaSatirPozr, solTLTutar, GenTLTutar
Sub PrintForm()
	If not IsObject(CKSN22E) Then
		Set MainTableE = Doc.GetTableObject("CKSN10E")
		Set MainTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("CKSN10T")
	Else
		Set MainTableE = Doc.GetTableObject("CKSN22E")
		Set MainTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("CKSN22T")
	End if

	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210

	Set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Fatura", sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")

	satirYuksekligi = 4.3

	Redim xCizgi(0)

	Ustmargin = 2 * satirYuksekligi
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 17 * satirYuksekligi

	solmargin = 6
	sagMargin = SayfaEni - 6
	satirpozr = 0

	sayfaSatirsayisi = 30
	sirano = 0
	sayfaSatirPozr = UstMargin + 9 * satirYuksekligi
	MainTableT.SafePack

	satirSayisi = MainTableT.GetRecCount
	Call Alanlar()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	Call PrintFormSayfaSonu(pe1)
	Call SayfaSayisiYazdir(pe1)
	
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	satirPozr = 63
	SatirSonu = 260
	SonSatir = 0
	Sutuny = 63
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Firma = Doc.Customp_GetS("FNAMEL0")
	res = pe1.Setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin, solMargin, 5, sagMargin - solMargin, trim(Firma))
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 6, solMargin, 5, sagMargin - solMargin, "Ç E K   Ç I K I Þ   B O R D R O S U")

	res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.SetItalic(0)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.PenColor(RGB(180, 180, 180))

	CHKodu = MainTableE.CIKISCODE
	Unvan1 = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", CHKodu, "")
	Unvan2 = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00", "NAME2", CHKodu, "")
	Adres1 = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00", "ADRES_1", CHKodu, "")
	Adres2 = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00", "ADRES_2", CHKodu, "")
	Adres3 = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00", "ADRES_3", CHKodu, "")
	VergiDairesi = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00", "VERGIDAIRESI", CHKodu, "")
	VergiNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "VERGINO", CHKodu, "")

	BBasla = 30
	KutuEn = 100
	res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 1, 5, KutuEn, CHKodu)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, KutuEn, Unvan1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, KutuEn, Unvan2)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, KutuEn, Adres1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, KutuEn, Adres2)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, KutuEn, Adres3)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, KutuEn, "Vergi Dairesi : " & VergiDairesi & " Vergi No : " & VergiNo)

	res = pe1.Box(BBasla - 2, solMargin, pe1.GetBottom - BBasla + 2, KutuEn + 2)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(sayfaSatirPozr + satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 136, 10, 20, "TARÝH")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, 25, ": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(MainTableE.TARIH))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 136, 10, 20, "BORDRO NO")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, 25, ": " & trim(MainTableE.EVRAKNO))

	Sol = SolMargin + 1
	res = pe1.Setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid, 1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i, 1)
		Baslik = Grid(i, 2)
		res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 3 * satirYuksekligi, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 2 , Baslik)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		xCizgi(uBound(XCizgi)) = sol - 1
		Redim Preserve xCizgi(uBound(XCizgi) + 1)
	Next
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
	If trim(MainTableT.E_CKSNNO(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
	Degisken = ""
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
	If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
		Call PrintFormSayfaSonu(pe1)
		siraNo = 1
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic
	Else
		If satirNo = 1 Then
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			satirpozr = SatirBaslangic + siraNo * satiryuksekligi
		End If
		siraNo = siraNo + 1
	End If		
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Sol = SolMargin + 1
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid, 1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i, 1) 
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i, 0)				
		if degiskenAdi = "_BANKASUBE" then
			Degisken = trim(MainTableT.E_BANKA(satirNo)) & " / " & trim(MainTableT.E_SUBE(satirNo))
		Elseif degiskenAdi = "_SEHIR" then
			Degisken = Doc.GetGecoValue("SEHIR","AD",MainTableT.E_SEHIR(satirNo),-1)
		Elseif degiskenAdi = "_CEKHESAPNO" then
			Degisken = trim(MainTableT.E_OREVRAKNO(satirNo)) & " / " & trim(MainTableT.E_HESAPNO(satirNo))
		else
			Degisken = MainTableT.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi, SatirNo)
		end if
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		if degiskenAdi = "E_OR_TUTAR" then
			solTutar = sol
			GenTutar = SutunGenisligi
		Elseif degiskenAdi = "E_TL_TUTAR" then
			solTLTutar = sol
			GenTLTutar = SutunGenisligi
		Elseif degiskenAdi = "E_OR_KUR" then
			solPB = sol
			GenPB = SutunGenisligi
		end if
		If Grid(i, 4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i, 6)
			Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18, Ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i, 4) = "D" Then
			Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
		End If		
		res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 2, Degisken)			
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
	Next					 
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub
Sub PrintFormSayfaSonu(pe1)
	satirUst = satirBaslangic -  4 * satirYuksekligi
	satirSonu = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi
'dýþ çerçeve
	res = pe1.PenColor(RGB(180, 180, 180))
	res = pe1.box(satirUst, SolMargin, satirSonu - satirUst, SagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - satirYuksekligi, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)

'dikey çizgiler
	For i = 0 To UBound(xCizgi) - 2
		LineX = cint(xCizgi(i))
		If lineX = 0 Then Exit For
		if i <> 6 then
			res = pe1.Line(satirUst, LineX, satirSonu - satirUst, 0)
		end if
	Next

	sBAsla = satirSonu + satirYuksekligi
	res = pe1.line(sBAsla, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	ToplamTutar = MainTableE.TL_TOPLAMTUTAR

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 1, solMargin + 1, 5, 30, "TOPLAM ADET")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, 30, ": "& MainTableT.GetRecCount)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 1, 5, 30, "BORDRO TOPLAMI")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 8, 2, ":")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	ToplamBasla = pe1.GetTop
	Set ToplamTable = BordroToplamTable()
	if ToplamTable.GetRecCount = 1 Then
	 	res = pe1.Write(ToplamBasla, solTutar, 5, GenTutar - 2, Doc.NumFormat(ToplamTable.E_OR_TUTAR(1),20,Grid(6,6)))
	 	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solPB, 5, GenPB - 2, ToplamTable.E_OR_KUR(1))
	Else
		ToplamBasla = ToplamBasla - satirYuksekligi
		for i = 1 to ToplamTable.GetRecCount
		 	res = pe1.Write(ToplamBasla + (i-1) * satirYuksekligi, solTutar, 5, GenTutar - 2, Doc.NumFormat(ToplamTable.E_OR_TUTAR(i),20,Grid(6,6)))
		 	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solPB, 5, GenPB - 2, ToplamTable.E_OR_KUR(i))
		Next
	End if
 	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solTLTutar, 5, GenTLTutar - 2, Doc.NumFormat(ToplamTutar,20,Grid(8,6)))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	PB = Doc.DefaultPB
' 	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solPB, 5, 20, Doc.DefaultPB)

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 1, 5, 30, "ORTALAMA VADE")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, 25, ": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(MainTableE.ORTALAMARVALOR))
	
	
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 1, 5, 30, "AÇIKLAMA")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, 45, ": " & (MainTableE.NOTES))
	

	res = pe1.line(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)

	Yalniz = Doc.NumToText_TL(ToplamTutar)
	if trim(PB) = trim(Doc.DefaultPB) then
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 1, 5, sagMargin - solMargin, "YALNIZ " & Yalniz & " hesabýnýza borç kaydedilmiþtir.")
	end if

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 1, 5, 52, "YETKÝLÝ")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 75, 5, 52, "DÜZENLEYEN")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 139, 5, 52, "TESLÝM ALAN")
	alt = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4 * satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 1, 5, 52, "LÜTFEN FAKSLAYINIZ")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solmargin + 139, 5, 52, "ÖDEMEYÝ EKSÝKSÝZ")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 52, "TESLÝM ALDIM")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 52, "KAÞE - ÝMZA")

	res = pe1.PenColor(RGB(180, 180, 180))

	res = pe1.line(alt, solMargin + 1, 0, 40)
	res = pe1.line(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 75, 0, 40)
	res = pe1.line(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 139, 0, 40)
End Sub
Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(8, 7)

	Grid(0, 0) = "E_CKSNNO"
	Grid(1, 0) = "E_KESIDECI"
	Grid(2, 0) = "_BANKASUBE"
	Grid(3, 0) = "_SEHIR"
	Grid(4, 0) = "_CEKHESAPNO"
	Grid(5, 0) = "E_RVADE"
	Grid(6, 0) = "E_OR_TUTAR"
	Grid(7, 0) = "E_OR_KUR"
	Grid(8, 0) = "E_TL_TUTAR"

	Grid(0, 1) = 12
	Grid(1, 1) = 37
	Grid(2, 1) = 34
	Grid(3, 1) = 19
	Grid(4, 1) = 24
	Grid(5, 1) = 17
	Grid(6, 1) = 26
	Grid(7, 1) = 8
	Grid(8, 1) = 20

	Grid(0, 2) = "ÇEK NO"
	Grid(1, 2) = "KEÞÝDECÝ"
	Grid(2, 2) = "BANKA / ÞUBE"
	Grid(3, 2) = "ÞEHÝR"
	Grid(4, 2) = "BANKA ÇEK / HESAP NO"
	Grid(5, 2) = "VADE TARÝHÝ"
	Grid(6, 2) = "TUTARI"
	Grid(7, 2) = ""
	Grid(8, 2) = Doc.DefaultPB & " TUTAR"

	Grid(0, 4) = "C"
	Grid(1, 4) = "C"
	Grid(2, 4) = "C"
	Grid(3, 4) = "C"
	Grid(4, 4) = "C"
	Grid(5, 4) = "D"
	Grid(6, 4) = "N"
	Grid(7, 4) = "C"
	Grid(8, 4) = "N"

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid, 1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i, 0)
		If Grid(i, 4) = "N" Then
			ondalik = MainTableT.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			Grid(i, 6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
	Grid(6, 6) = 2
	Grid(8, 6) = 2
End Sub
Sub SayfaSayisiYazdir(pe1)
	for i = 1 to pe1.GetPageCount()
		res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(sayfaSatirPozr, solMargin + 136, 5, 20, "SAYFA")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, 25, ": " & i & " / " & pe1.GetPageCount)
	Next
End Sub
Function BordroToplamTable()
	AppStr = MainTableT.GetAppName()
	Set C10T = Doc.Select2("XXX", AppStr, "", "", -1)
	C10T.GroupBy2("E_OR_KUR")
	Set BordroToplamTable = C10T
End Function

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : HAK6_2014 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : STOK74-01
Hangi Tablo için : STOK74
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : L2 SATIN ALMA FATURA
Kod : 
Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,Tip, SolMargin, SagMargin, Ustmargin, AltMargin, FormBaslik, EnAlt
Public AlanSayisi()	, satirYuksekligi, TopTLTutar, ToplamMiktar, TopOrjTutar
Public xCizgi()
Public Grid
Sub PrintForm()
	ReDim xCizgi(1)
	ToplamEn = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	EnAlt = 0
	TopTLTutar = 0
	TopOrjTutar = 0
	ToplamMiktar=0
	
	satiryuksekligi = 5.5
'***
	FormBaslik = "L2 SATIN ALMA HESAPLAMA TABLOSU"
	VPEAdi = FormBaslik
	MustEnter = "KOD"
'***
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	For i = STOK74T.GetRecCount() To 1 Step -1
		If trim(STOK74T.GetFieldValue(MustEnter,i)) <> "" Then
			satirSayisi_tableT = i 
			Exit For
		ElseIf i = 1 Then
			satirSayisi_tableT = 1 
		End If
	Next

	sayfaEni = 297
	sayfaBoyu = 210

	res = pe1.Open(0, VPEAdi, SayfaEni, SayfaBoyu, "LANDSCAPE")

	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	UstMargin = 10
	AltMargin = sayfaBoyu - 15
	satirSonu = AltMargin - 45
	Call Alanlar()

	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	
	Dim satirSayisi_tableT
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For I = 1 To satirSayisi_tableT
			Call Print_form_satir(pe1, I)
	Next	
	
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.setalign("right")
	
	res = pe1.Line(147, 10, 0, SayfaEni-20)
	 
	res = pe1.Write(148, xCizgi(0)+10 , 6,30," Toplam Miktar (Kg) : ")
	res = pe1.Write(148, xCizgi(1)+2 , 6,20,doc.NumFormat(ToplamMiktar,8,2))
	
	res = pe1.Write(160, xCizgi(0)+5 , 6,30," Valör Tarihi : ")
	res = pe1.Write(160, xCizgi(1) , 6,20, Doc.StrtoNordate(STOK74E.VALORTARIH))
	
	
	if TopTLTutar=TopOrjTutar then
			TopOrjTutar = 	(TopTLTutar) / (STOK74E.CH_KURV)
			a=doc.NumFormat(STOK74E.CH_KURV(1),8,4)
			res = pe1.Write(155, xCizgi(1) , 6,180," Satýn Alma TL olarak yapýlmýþ olup Toplam Döviz Tutarý 1 $ ="& a &" TL üzerinden hesaplanmýþtýr.")
	end if		
	
	res = pe1.Write(148, xCizgi(5)+5 , 6,35," Toplam Tutar : ")
	res = pe1.Write(148, xCizgi(7) , 6,20, doc.NumFormat(TopTLTutar,8,2))
	res = pe1.Write(148, xCizgi(8) , 6,20, doc.NumFormat(TopOrjTutar,8,2))
	

	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	Call SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)

	res = pe1.nopen()
	
	If Tip = "LANDSCAPE" Then
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 20)
		res = pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
		res = pe1.write(Ustmargin + 8, SolMargin, 15, SagMargin - SolMargin, FormBaslik)
	Else
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 20)
		res = pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
		res = pe1.write(Ustmargin + 8, SolMargin, 15, SagMargin - SolMargin, FormBaslik)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	End If
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", "10")

	
	BBasla = UstMargin + 10
	SatirAra = 6
'*******************
	Blok10 = SolMargin + 2
	Blok11 = SolMargin + 15
	Blok20 = SolMargin + 235
	Blok21 = SolMargin + 255
		
	Genislik10 = Blok11 - Blok10 - 1
	Genislik20 = Blok21 - Blok20 - 1
	
	Genislik11 = Blok20 - Blok11 - 1
	Genislik21 = SagMargin - Blok21 - 1
	

	BBasla = 32
	SatirAra = 6
	
	FisNo = STOK74E.EVRAKNO(1)
	Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(STOK74E.TARIH(1))
	CHAdi = trim(STOK74E.CH_KODU_CARI00_AD(1))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla , Blok10, 6, Genislik10, "Fiþ No : ")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla , Blok11, 6, Genislik11, ": " & trim(FisNo))
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla, Blok20, 6, Genislik20, "Giriþ Tarihi : ")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla, Blok21, 6, Genislik21, ": " & Tarih)
		
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)		
	res = pe1.write(BBasla + SatirAra, Blok10, 6, Genislik10, "Firma")		
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla + SatirAra, Blok11, 6, Genislik11, ": " & Trim(CHAdi) )
		
'***********************		
	satirBaslangic = pe1.GetBottom() + 15
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Sol = SolMargin + 1
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		Baslik = Grid(i,2)
'		---- sütun baþlýklarýnýn yazýldýðý yer ---
		If SutunGenisligi > 0 Then	
			res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 13, Sol, 18, SutunGenisligi - 2 , Baslik)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			xCizgi(uBound(xCizgi)-1) = Sol - 1		
			ReDim Preserve xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi) + 1)		
		End If
	Next

	res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
'yatay çizgiler
	res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
	res = pe1.Line(28, 10, 0, SayfaEni-20)
	'res = pe1.Line(131, 10, 0, SayfaEni-20) -- bu satýrý 257.satýra kopyaladým.
	res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 14, 10, 0, sayfaEni -20)
	res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 2, 10, 0, sayfaEni -20)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(1001)
	res = pe1.PenColor(RGB(0, 0, 0))        
	res = pe1.pensize(0.5)

	Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
	If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
		EnAlt = pe1.GetBottom()
	'res = pe1.Write(162, xCizgi(5) -27, 6,50,"ARA TOPLAM MÝKTAR")
	
	'--------------------------------------------------------------------------
	
		For i = 0 To (UBound(xCizgi) -1)
			LineX = cint(xCizgi(i))
					If LineX = 0 Then Exit For
	   '	-- ilk sayfada dikey çizgilerin indirileceði yer---------
	   
				res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 14, LineX, 125 , 0)
	   
		Next
	
		'--------------------------------------------------------------------------
	
	
	res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 14, LineX, EnAlt - satirBaslangic +50 , 0)
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
	  ReDim xCizgi(1)
	  res = pe1.Line(131, 10, 0, SayfaEni-20)
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic
	Else
		If satirNo = 1 Then
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
		End If
	End If
	siraNo = siraNo + 1
		
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Sol = SolMargin + 1
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	
	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
		If Left(DegiskenAdi,1) <> "_" Then
			Degisken = STOK74T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)
			If  degiskenAdi = "SF_MIKTAR" Then
					ToplamMiktar= ToplamMiktar + Degisken
					
			ElseIf  degiskenAdi = "TL_TUTAR" Then
					TopTLTutar= TopTLTutar + Degisken	
					
			ElseIf  degiskenAdi = "OR_TUTAR" Then
					TopOrjTutar= TopOrjTutar + Degisken			
					
			End If
			
		Else
			If DegiskenAdi = "_SIRANO" Then
				Degisken = SiraNo
			End If
		End If
		
			
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,Ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
		End If
		
	res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 2, Degisken)
	
 
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
	Next		
	
			
				 
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub


Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
'dýþ çerçeve
		enalt=170
	If EnAlt = 0 Or EnAlt > SayfaBoyu Then EnAlt = AltMargin 

	res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
	'-- en alt çizgi --
	res = pe1.Line(EnAlt , SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)
	
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	'TopTLTutar = Doc.NumFormat(TopTLTutar,20,Grid(7,6))
	'TopOrjTutar = Doc.NumFormat(TopOrjTutar,20,Grid(8,6))
	'ToplamMiktar = Doc.NumFormat(ToplamMiktar,20,Grid(1,6))
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		
	SatirAra = 10
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)

	
	OnaySatir = Baslangic + 175
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.Write(OnaySatir , SolMargin + 5, 6,75," HAZIRLAYAN : KADER ÇAKMAK")
	res = pe1.Write(OnaySatir , SolMargin + 205, 6,75,"ONAY : ")
	KutuAlt = pe1.GetBottom() + 10

	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	
	
	For i = 0 To UBound(xCizgi) -1
		LineX = cint(xCizgi(i))
		If LineX = 0 Then Exit For
	   '	-- dikey çizgilerin indirileceði yer---------
	   
	   res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 14, LineX, EnAlt - satirBaslangic - 10 , 0)
	   
	Next
	
	res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
	res = pe1.box(UstMargin - 2, SolMargin, KutuAlt - UstMargin + 2, SagMargin - SolMargin)
	
	
'dikey çizgiler
End Sub

Sub SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	For i = 1 To pe1.GetPageCount()
		res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 2, SagMargin - 40,5,12,"Sayfa")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 2, SagMargin - 28,5,27,":  " & i & "  /  " & pe1.GetPageCount())
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Next
End Sub
Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(10,6)
	
	Grid(0,0) = "_SIRANO"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(3,0) = "KTA_LMEORT"
	Grid(4,0) = "KTA_PREMIUM"
	Grid(5,0) = "OR_FIYAT"
	Grid(6,0) = "KUR"
	Grid(7,0) = "TL_FIYAT"
	Grid(8,0) = "TL_TUTAR"
	Grid(9,0) = "OR_TUTAR" 
	
	Grid(0,1) = 9
	Grid(1,1) = 70
	Grid(2,1) = 22	
	Grid(3,1) = 22
	Grid(4,1) = 22	
	Grid(5,1) = 22	
	Grid(6,1) = 22
	Grid(7,1) = 22	
	Grid(8,1) = 30
	Grid(9,1) = 30
		
	Grid(0,2) = "Sýra No"			
	Grid(1,2) = "Mamul Cinsi"						
	Grid(2,2) = "Miktar (Kg)"
	Grid(3,2) = "LME ORT"				
	Grid(4,2) = "Premium"		
	Grid(5,2) = "Birim Fiyat USD / Kg"				
	Grid(6,2) = "Kur Deðeri"				
	Grid(7,2) = "Birim Fiyat TL / Kg"			
	Grid(8,2) = "TL Tutar"					
	Grid(9,2) = "USD Tutar"
	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"	
	Grid(1,4) = "C"	
	Grid(2,4) = "N"	
	Grid(3,4) = "N"	
	Grid(4,4) = "N"	
 Grid(5,4) = "N"	
	Grid(6,4) = "N"	
	Grid(7,4) = "N"	
	Grid(8,4) = "N"	
	Grid(9,4) = "N"	
	
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = STOK74T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik
		End If
	Next	
	Grid(2, 6) = 2
	Grid(3, 6) = 2
	Grid(4, 6) = 2
	Grid(5, 6) = 4
	Grid(6, 6) = 4
	Grid(7, 6) = 4
	Grid(8, 6) = 2
	Grid(9, 6) = 2
		
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	res = pe1.setitalic(0)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
	res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
	res = pe1.setrotation(0)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.setalign("left")
End Sub

Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satiryuksekligi
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,Tip, satirAra, SatirBitis
Public AlanSayisi(), ToplamTutar, ToplamProTutar
Public xCizgi()
Public Secilenler()
Public Grid()

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 297
	
	ToplamEn = 0
	ToplamTutar = 0
	ToplamProTutar = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	UstMargin = 15
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 10 
	SolMargin = 12
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	satiryuksekligi = 5
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 60
	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 10 * satirYuksekligi

	ReDim xCizgi(0)
	
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satinalma Siparisi",  SayfaEni, SayfaBoyu,"LANDSCAPE")
	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	siraNo = 0        
	satirSayisi = STOK46T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
			Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
		
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
'---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		res = pe1.nopen()
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 20)
		res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 3,SolMargin,8,SagMargin - SolMargin,"SATINALMA SÝPARÝÞ FORMU")
		logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
		res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin - 3,SolMargin+5,18,60,logo,114)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	
	'--- firma adýný ekledim-----	
	FirmaAdi = Doc.Customp_Gets("FNAMEL0")	
	res = pe1.SetAlign("right")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin , SolMargin+175, 6, 100, Trim(FirmaAdi))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("center")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 4 , SolMargin+190, 6, 90, "Barýþ Mah. Anibal Cad. No:25/A Gebze 41400 KOCAELÝ")
	'res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 6 , SolMargin+170, 6, 100, "")
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 8 , SolMargin+190, 6, 90, "Tel : 0 262 643 20 92 Pbx - Fax : 0 262 642 77 86")
	'res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 12 , SolMargin+170, 6, 100, "")
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 12 , SolMargin+190, 6, 90, "www.hak.com.tr")
	'-------

		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
		SiparisNo = STOK46E.EVRAKNO(1)
		Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(STOK46E.TARIH(1))
		CHKodu = STOK46E.CH_KODU(1)
		CHAdi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","AD",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHAdi2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","NAME2",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHAdi = trim(CHAdi) & " " & trim(CHAdi2)
		Adres1 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres3 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",CHKodu,-1) 
		Faks = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","FAXNO",CHKodu,-1)
				
		SiparisiAlan = STOK46E.SIPIALAN(1)
		SiparisiVeren = STOK46E.SIPIVEREN(1)

		bBasla = UstMargin+17
		SatirAra = 6
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 2, 6,33,"TEDARÝKÇÝ FÝRMA")
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 36, 6,2,": ")
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 38, 6,143,CHAdi)

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,33,"ADRESÝ")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + SatirAra, SolMargin + 36,6,2,": ")
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + SatirAra, SolMargin + 38,6,85,Adres1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 38, 6,85,Adres2)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 38, 6,85,Adres3)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,33,"FAX NO")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 36, 6,2,": ")
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 38, 6,85,Faks)

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 182, 6,15, "TARÝH")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 212, 6,50,": " & Tarih)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + SatirAra, SolMargin + 182, 6,26, "SÝPARÝÞ NO")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + SatirAra, SolMargin + 212, 6,50,": " & trim(SiparisNo))
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 182, 6,26, "ÖDEME ÞEKLÝ")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 212,6,50, ": " & trim(STOK46E.ODEMEGUNU(1)) & " gün")
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 182, 6,30, "NAKLÝYE ÞEKLÝ")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 212,6,50, ": " & trim(STOK46E.NAKLIYECIFIRMA(1)))

	' Sütun baþlýklarý
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
 		res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
			If i = 3 Or i = 5 Or i = 7 Then
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) + Grid(i+1,1)
			Else
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			End If
			Baslik = Grid(i,2)
			res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 12, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 4 , Baslik)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		Next
		res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
		Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub
'---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
	'res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(0)
	'res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(255,0,0))

	Dim satirpozr
	If trim(STOK46T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
	Degisken = ""
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
	If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		siraNo = 1
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic
	Else
		If satirNo = 1 Then
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			'satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
			satirpozr = SatirBaslangic + siraNo * satiryuksekligi
		End If
		siraNo = siraNo + 1
	End If		
	
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Sol = SolMargin + 1
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	
	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
		Degisken = trim(cstr(STOK46T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20, Ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
		End If

		If Grid(i,0) = "_NOTLAR12" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
			Not1 = Trim(cstr(STOK46T.GetFieldValue("NOTE0", SatirNo)))
			Not2 = Trim(cstr(STOK46T.GetFieldValue("NOTE1", SatirNo)))
			Degisken = Not1 & " " & Not2 
		End If
		
		res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 2, Degisken)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		If satirNo = 1 Then
			xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)) = Sol - 1
			ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
		End If
	Next					 
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub
'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	'dýþ çerçeve
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.8)
	res = pe1.box(UstMargin-4, SolMargin, AltMargin - UstMargin+4, SagMargin - SolMargin)

	'üst çizgiler
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin + 16, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)		
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)

	'dikey çizgiler
	SatirBitis = SatirBaslangic + 10 * satiryuksekligi
	cizgiBoyu = SatirBitis - UstMargin - 47.5
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)-1
		If i <> 5 And i <> 3 Then
			LineX = xCizgi(i)		
			res = pe1.Line(UstMargin+48.5, LineX, cizgiBoyu, 0)
		End If
	Next
	
	'notlarla toplam arasýndaki dikey çizgi
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.5)
	'res = pe1.Line(UstMargin+47.5 + cizgiBoyu,xCizgi(4) , 27, 0)
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.8)
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin+74.5+cizgiBoyu, xCizgi(2) ,AltMargin-UstMargin-74.5-cizgiBoyu ,0)
	
	'kutu çiz- lütfen onaylayýn kutusu
	res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(0)
	res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(0,0,0))
	res = pe1.box(UstMargin+74.5+cizgiBoyu, xCizgi(2), 8, SagMargin-xCizgi(2))
	res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(1)
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SetTextColor(RGB(255,255,255))
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin+76.5+cizgiBoyu, xCizgi(2)+2, 5, SagMargin-xCizgi(2)-4, "LÜTFEN FORMU ONAYLAYARAK FÝRMAMIZA GERÝ FAKSLAYINIZ ")
	res = pe1.SetTextColor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	
	'yatay çizgiler
	'res = pe1.Line(UstMargin + 21, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)		
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.8)
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin+47.5, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin-SolMargin)		
	res = pe1.Line(SatirBitis+27, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin-SolMargin)		
	res = pe1.pensize(0.5)
	res = pe1.Line(SatirBitis, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin-SolMargin)		
	res = pe1.Line(SatirBaslangic - 2, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin-SolMargin)		

	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)

	'notlar mesajlar
	rows = Doc.SelectEQ("GNMESG2","KRITER+TRNUM","PO  "+STOK46E.EVRAKNO(1)+Space(6))
	Set Old = Doc.GetTableObject("AU_GNMESG2_SELECT")
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	
	'res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(0)
	'res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(255,0,0))
	
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+2 ,Solmargin+2, 5, 20, "NOTLAR")
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis+2 ,Solmargin+22, 5, 3, ":")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res=pe1.setbold(0)
	satirpozr=SatirBitis + 2 - SatirYuksekligi
	
	Not1 = Trim(STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1(1))
	Not2 = Trim(STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2(1))
	Not3 = Trim(STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_3(1))
	Not4 = Trim(STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_4(1))
	
	If trim(Not1) <> "" Then satirpozr=satirpozr+SatirYuksekligi
	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, SolMargin+25, 6, SagMargin-SolMargin-70, Not1)
	If trim(Not2) <> "" Then satirpozr=satirpozr+SatirYuksekligi
	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, SolMargin+25, 6, SagMargin-SolMargin-70, Not2)
	If trim(Not3) <> "" Then satirpozr=satirpozr+SatirYuksekligi
	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, SolMargin+25, 6, SagMargin-SolMargin-70, Not3)	
	If trim(Not4) <> "" Then satirpozr=satirpozr+SatirYuksekligi
	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, SolMargin+25, 6, SagMargin-SolMargin-70, Not4)	
		
	If rows > 3 And pe1.GetPageCount() = 1 Then msgbox "En fazla 3 satýr mesaj yazabilirsiniz"
	
	For i = 1 To rows
		If i > 3 Then Exit For
		Mesaj = Old.NOTES(i)
		If trim(Mesaj) <> "" Then  satirpozr=satirpozr+SatirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr,Solmargin + 25, 5, SagMargin - SolMargin - 120,Mesaj)		
	Next

	Ondalik = Grid(7,6)
	ToplamTutar = STOK46E.OR_NEWTOPLAM(1)
	IskontoOrani = STOK46E.DO_ISKONTOX_ORANI(1)
	IskontoToplami=STOK46E.OR_ISKONTOLARTOPLAMI(1)
	NetTutar = STOK46E.OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM(1)
	KDVTutari = STOK46E.OR_KDVLERTOPLAM(1)
	KDVDahilTutar = STOK46E.OR_KDVDAHILTOPLAM(1)
	KDVOrani = STOK46E.DO_KDV_ORANI(1)

	ToplamTutar = Doc.NumFormat(ToplamTutar,18, Ondalik)
	IskontoOrani = Doc.NumFormat(IskontoOrani,18, Ondalik)
	IskontoToplami = Doc.NumFormat(IskontoToplami,18, Ondalik)
	NetTutar = Doc.NumFormat(NetTutar,18, Ondalik)
	KDVOrani = Doc.NumFormat(KDVOrani,18, Ondalik)
	KDVTutari = Doc.NumFormat(KDVTutari,18, Ondalik)
	KDVDahilTutar = Doc.NumFormat(KDVDahilTutar,18, Ondalik)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	
	sss = SatirYuksekligi

	SatirYuksekligi = 4.7

	'res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2,xCizgi(4) + 3,6,30,"TOPLAM")
	'res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2 + satirYuksekligi,xCizgi(4) + 3,6,40,"ÝSKONTO %  " & IskontoOrani)
	'res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2 + 2 * satirYuksekligi,xCizgi(4) + 3,6,30,"NET TOPLAM")
	'res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2 + 3 * satirYuksekligi,xCizgi(4) + 3,6,40,"KDV %  " & KDVOrani)
	'res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	'res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	'res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2.5 + 4 * satirYuksekligi,xCizgi(4) + 3,6,30,"GENEL TOPLAM")

	'res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	'res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2,275,6,10,STOK46E.PRICEUNIT(1))
	'res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2 + satirYuksekligi,275,6,10,STOK46E.PRICEUNIT(1))
	'res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2 + 2 * satirYuksekligi,275,6,10,STOK46E.PRICEUNIT(1))
	'res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2 + 3 * satirYuksekligi,275,6,10,STOK46E.PRICEUNIT(1))

	'res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	'res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2.5 + 4 * satirYuksekligi,275,6,10,STOK46E.PRICEUNIT(1))

	'res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	'res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	'res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2,xCizgi(6) + 2,6,xCizgi(7) - xCizgi(6) - 4, ToplamTutar)
	'res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2 + satirYuksekligi,xCizgi(6) + 2,6,xCizgi(7) - xCizgi(6) - 4, IskontoToplami)
	'res = pe1.Line(SatirBitis + 2 + 2 * satirYuksekligi - 1,xCizgi(6),0,xCizgi(8) - xCizgi(6))
	'res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2 + 2 * satirYuksekligi,xCizgi(6) + 2,6,xCizgi(7) - xCizgi(6) - 4, NetTutar)
	'res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2 + 3 * satirYuksekligi,xCizgi(6) + 2,12,xCizgi(7) - xCizgi(6) - 4, KDVTutari)
	'res = pe1.Line(SatirBitis + 2 + 4 * satirYuksekligi - 1,xCizgi(6),0,xCizgi(8) - xCizgi(6))
	'res = pe1.Line(SatirBitis + 2 + 4 * satirYuksekligi,xCizgi(6),0,xCizgi(8) - xCizgi(6))
	'res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	'res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	'res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2.5 + 4 * satirYuksekligi,xCizgi(6) + 2,6,xCizgi(7) - xCizgi(6) - 4, KDVDahilTutar)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	
	SatirYuksekligi = sss
	GAlt = pe1.GetBottom() - 5
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+76.5+cizgiBoyu, SolMargin+2, 6, xCizgi(2)-SolMargin-4, "GENEL ÞARTLAR")

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")	
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	res = pe1.WriteRTFFile(UstMargin+76.5+cizgiBoyu+5, SolMargin + 2,38,xCizgi(2) - SolMargin - 4, "..\doc\genel.rtf")

	GAlt = pe1.GetBottom()
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+85+cizgiBoyu, 200, 6, 40, "SÝPARÝÞÝ VEREN")
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+85+cizgiBoyu, 240, 6, 40, "SÝPARÝÞÝ ALAN")

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin+82.5 + cizgiBoyu, xCizgi(4), AltMargin - UstMargin - 82.5 - cizgiBoyu ,0)
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin+82.5 + cizgiBoyu, 238, AltMargin-UstMargin-82.5 - cizgiBoyu ,0)
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin+90.5 + cizgiBoyu, xCizgi(4), 0,sagMargin - xCizgi(4))

	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+94.5 + cizgiBoyu, xCizgi(2) + 2,6,30, "ADI SOYADI")
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+100.5 + cizgiBoyu, xCizgi(2) + 2,6,30, "TARÝH")
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+106.5 + cizgiBoyu, xCizgi(2) + 2,6,30, "KAÞE-ÝMZA")

	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+94.5 + cizgiBoyu, xCizgi(4) + 2, 6, xCizgi(6) - xCizgi(4) - 4, STOK46E.SIPIVEREN(1))
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+94.5 + cizgiBoyu, xCizgi(6) + 2, 6, xCizgi(8) - xCizgi(6) - 4, STOK46E.SIPIALAN(1))
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+100.5 + cizgiBoyu, xCizgi(4) + 2, 6, xCizgi(6) - xCizgi(4) - 4, Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK46E.TARIH(1)))

	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin +0.5,SagMargin - 35,6,35,"FR SA 002/1")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
End Sub
'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(9,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2,0) = "RTESTARIH"
	Grid(3,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(4,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(5,0) = "_NOTLAR12"
	'Grid(6,0) = "PRICEUNIT"
	'Grid(7,0) = "OR_TUTAR"
	'Grid(8,0) = "PRICEUNIT"

	Grid(0,1) = 51
	Grid(1,1) = 85
	Grid(2,1) = 20
	Grid(3,1) = 16
	Grid(4,1) = 14
	Grid(5,1) = 89
	'Grid(6,1) = 14
	'Grid(7,1) = 32
	'Grid(8,1) = 14

	Grid(0,2) = "MALZEME KODU"
	Grid(1,2) = "MALZEME ADI"
	Grid(2,2) = "TESLÝM TARÝHÝ"
	Grid(3,2) = "MÝKTAR ve BÝRÝM"
	Grid(4,2) = ""
	Grid(5,2) = "NOT"
	'Grid(6,2) = ""
	'Grid(7,2) = "TUTAR"
	'Grid(8,2) = ""
	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "D"
	Grid(3,4) = "N"
	Grid(4,4) = "C"
	Grid(5,4) = "N"
	'Grid(6,4) = "C"
	'Grid(7,4) = "N"
	'Grid(8,4) = "C"
	
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i,0)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			'ondalik = DecimalHesapla(Degisken)	
			ondalik = STOK46T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
			If i = 7 Then
				ToplamTutar = STOK46E.OR_NEWTOPLAM(1)
				dToplamTutar = Doc.SigDig(ToplamTutar)
				IskontoToplami=STOK46E.OR_ISKONTOLARTOPLAMI(1)
				dIskontoToplami = Doc.SigDig(IskontoToplami)
				NetTutar = STOK46E.OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM(1)
				dNetTutar = Doc.SigDig(NetTutar)
				KDVTutari = STOK46E.OR_KDVLERTOPLAM(1)
				dKDVTutari = Doc.SigDig(KDVTutari)
				KDVDahilTutar = STOK46E.OR_KDVDAHILTOPLAM(1)
				dKDVDahilTutar = Doc.SigDig(KDVDahilTutar)

				Ondalik1 = Ondalik 
				If dIskontoToplami > Ondalik1 Then
					Ondalik1 = dIskontoToplami
				ElseIf dNetTutar > Ondalik1 Then
					Ondalik1 = dNetTutar
				ElseIf dKDVTutari > Ondalik1 Then
					Ondalik1 = dKDVTutari
				ElseIf dKDVDahilTutar > Ondalik1 Then
					Ondalik1 = dKDVDahilTutar
				End If

				If Ondalik1 > 2 Then Ondalik1 = 2
				Grid(i,6) = Ondalik1
			End If
		End If
	Next	
	For i = 0 To uBound(Grid,1) - 1
		ToplamEn = ToplamEn + Grid(i,1)
	Next
End Sub
'------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Normal(pe1)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.setitalic(0)
		res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
		res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
		res = pe1.setrotation(0)
		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
		res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : HAK6_2014 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : CEKGBR
Hangi Tablo için : CKSN20

Kod : 
Public ustmargin, solmargin, sagMargin, satirYuksekligi, sirano, MainTableE, MainTableT
Dim sayfaSatirsayisi, AraToplam, Grid, satirSonu, satirBaslangic, ssatirYuksekligi
Dim solTutar, GenTutar, solPB, GenPB, xCizgi(), sayfaSatirPozr, solTLTutar, GenTLTutar

Sub PrintForm()
	if not IsObject(CKSN20E) Then
		Set MainTableE = Doc.GetTableObject("CKSN10E")
		Set MainTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("CKSN10T")
	Else
		Set MainTableE = Doc.GetTableObject("CKSN20E")
		Set MainTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("CKSN20T")
	End if

	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210

	Set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Fatura", sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")

	satirYuksekligi = 4.3
	ssatirYuksekligi = 8
	Redim xCizgi(0)

	Ustmargin = 2 * satirYuksekligi
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 17 * satirYuksekligi

	solmargin = 6
	sagMargin = SayfaEni - 6
	satirpozr = 0
	sayfaSatirsayisi = 30
	sirano = 0
	sayfaSatirPozr = UstMargin + 9 * satirYuksekligi
	MainTableT.SafePack

	satirSayisi = MainTableT.GetRecCount
	Call Alanlar()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	Call PrintFormSayfaSonu(pe1)
	Call SayfaSayisiYazdir(pe1)
	
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	satirPozr = 63
	SatirSonu = 260
	SonSatir = 0
	Sutuny = 63
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Firma = Doc.Customp_GetS("FNAMEL0")
	res = pe1.Setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin, solMargin, 5, sagMargin - solMargin, trim(Firma))
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 6, solMargin, 5, sagMargin - solMargin, "Ç E K   G Ý R Ý Þ   B O R D R O S U")

	res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.SetItalic(0)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.PenColor(RGB(180, 180, 180))

	CHKodu = MainTableE.GIRISCODE
	Unvan1 = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", CHKodu, "")
	Unvan2 = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00", "NAME2", CHKodu, "")
	Adres1 = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00", "ADRES_1", CHKodu, "")
	Adres2 = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00", "ADRES_2", CHKodu, "")
	Adres3 = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00", "ADRES_3", CHKodu, "")
	VergiDairesi = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00", "VERGIDAIRESI", CHKodu, "")
	VergiNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "VERGINO", CHKodu, "")

	BBasla = 30
	KutuEn = 100
	res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 1, 5, KutuEn, CHKodu)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, KutuEn, Unvan1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, KutuEn, Unvan2)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, KutuEn, Adres1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, KutuEn, Adres2)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, KutuEn, Adres3)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, KutuEn, "Vergi Dairesi : " & VergiDairesi & " Vergi No : " & VergiNo)

	res = pe1.Box(BBasla - 2, solMargin, pe1.GetBottom - BBasla + 2, KutuEn + 2)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(sayfaSatirPozr + satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 136, 10, 20, "TARÝH")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, 25, ": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(MainTableE.TARIH))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 136, 10, 20, "BORDRO NO")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, 25, ": " & trim(MainTableE.EVRAKNO))

	Sol = SolMargin + 1
	res = pe1.Setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid, 1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i, 1)
		Baslik = Grid(i, 2)
		res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 3 * satirYuksekligi, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 2 , Baslik)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		xCizgi(uBound(XCizgi)) = sol - 1
		Redim Preserve xCizgi(uBound(XCizgi) + 1)
	Next
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
	If trim(MainTableT.E_CKSNNO(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
	Degisken = ""
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
	If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
		Call PrintFormSayfaSonu(pe1)
		siraNo = 1
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic
	Else
		If satirNo = 1 Then
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			satirpozr = SatirBaslangic + siraNo * ssatirYuksekligi
		End If
		siraNo = siraNo + 1
	End If		
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Sol = SolMargin + 1
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid, 1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i, 1) 
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i, 0)				
		if degiskenAdi = "_BANKASUBE" then
			Degisken = trim(MainTableT.E_BANKA(satirNo)) & " / " & trim(MainTableT.E_SUBE(satirNo))
		Elseif degiskenAdi = "_SEHIR" then
			Degisken = Doc.GetGecoValue("SEHIR","AD",MainTableT.E_SEHIR(satirNo),-1)
		Elseif degiskenAdi = "_CEKHESAPNO" then
			Degisken = trim(MainTableT.E_OREVRAKNO(satirNo)) & " / " & trim(MainTableT.E_HESAPNO(satirNo))
		else
			Degisken = MainTableT.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi, SatirNo)
		end if
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		if degiskenAdi = "E_OR_TUTAR" then
			solTutar = sol
			GenTutar = SutunGenisligi
		Elseif degiskenAdi = "E_TL_TUTAR" then
			solTLTutar = sol
			GenTLTutar = SutunGenisligi
		Elseif degiskenAdi = "E_OR_KUR" then
			solPB = sol
			GenPB = SutunGenisligi
		end if
		If Grid(i, 4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i, 6)
			Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18, Ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i, 4) = "D" Then
			Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
		End If		
		res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, ssatirYuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 1.5, Degisken)			
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
	Next					 
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub
Sub PrintFormSayfaSonu(pe1)
	satirUst = satirBaslangic -  4 * satirYuksekligi
	satirSonu = pe1.GetTop + ssatirYuksekligi
'dýþ çerçeve
	res = pe1.PenColor(RGB(180, 180, 180))
	res = pe1.box(satirUst, SolMargin, satirSonu - satirUst, SagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - satirYuksekligi, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)

'dikey çizgiler
	For i = 0 To UBound(xCizgi) - 2
		LineX = cint(xCizgi(i))
		If lineX = 0 Then Exit For
		if i <> 6 then
			res = pe1.Line(satirUst, LineX, satirSonu - satirUst, 0)
		end if
	Next

	sBAsla = satirSonu + satirYuksekligi
	res = pe1.line(sBAsla, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	ToplamTutar = MainTableE.TL_TOPLAMTUTAR

	Set ToplamTable = BordroToplamTable()
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 1, solMargin + 1, 5, 30, "TOPLAM ADET")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, 30, ": "& MainTableT.GetRecCount)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 1, 5, 30, "BORDRO TOPLAMI")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 8, 2, ":")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	ToplamBasla = pe1.GetTop
	if ToplamTable.GetRecCount = 1 Then
	 	res = pe1.Write(ToplamBasla, solTutar, 5, GenTutar - 2, Doc.NumFormat(ToplamTable.E_OR_TUTAR(1),20,Grid(6,6)))
	 	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solPB, 5, GenPB - 2, ToplamTable.E_OR_KUR(1))
	Else
		ToplamBasla = ToplamBasla - satirYuksekligi
		for i = 1 to ToplamTable.GetRecCount
		 	res = pe1.Write(ToplamBasla + (i-1) * satirYuksekligi, solTutar, 5, GenTutar - 2, Doc.NumFormat(ToplamTable.E_OR_TUTAR(i),20,Grid(6,6)))
		 	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solPB, 5, GenPB - 2, ToplamTable.E_OR_KUR(i))
		Next
	End if
 	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solTLTutar, 5, GenTLTutar - 2, Doc.NumFormat(ToplamTutar,20,Grid(8,6)))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	PB = Doc.DefaultPB
' 	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solPB, 5, 20, Doc.DefaultPB)

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 1, 5, 30, "ORTALAMA VADE")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, 25, ": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(MainTableE.ORTALAMARVALOR))
	
	
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 1, 5, 30, "AÇIKLAMA")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, 45, ": " & (MainTableE.NOTES))
	

	res = pe1.line(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)

	Yalniz = Doc.NumToText_TL(ToplamTutar)
	if trim(PB) = trim(Doc.DefaultPB) then
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 1, 5, sagMargin - solMargin, "YALNIZ " & Yalniz & " hesabýnýza alacak kaydedilmiþtir.")
	end if

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 1, 5, 52, "YETKÝLÝ")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 75, 5, 52, "DÜZENLEYEN")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 139, 5, 52, "TESLÝM ALAN")
	alt = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi

'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4 * satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 1, 5, 52, "LÜTFEN FAKSLAYINIZ")
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solmargin + 139, 5, 52, "ÖDEMEYÝ EKSÝKSÝZ")
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 52, "TESLÝM ALDIM")
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 52, "KAÞE - ÝMZA")

	res = pe1.PenColor(RGB(180, 180, 180))

	res = pe1.line(alt, solMargin + 1, 0, 40)
	res = pe1.line(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 75, 0, 40)
	res = pe1.line(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 139, 0, 40)
End Sub
Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(8, 7)

	Grid(0, 0) = "E_CKSNNO"
	Grid(1, 0) = "E_KESIDECI"
	Grid(2, 0) = "_BANKASUBE"
	Grid(3, 0) = "_SEHIR"
	Grid(4, 0) = "_CEKHESAPNO"
	Grid(5, 0) = "E_RVADE"
	Grid(6, 0) = "E_OR_TUTAR"
	Grid(7, 0) = "E_OR_KUR"
	Grid(8, 0) = "E_TL_TUTAR"

	Grid(0, 1) = 12
	Grid(1, 1) = 37
	Grid(2, 1) = 34
	Grid(3, 1) = 19
	Grid(4, 1) = 31
	Grid(5, 1) = 16
	Grid(6, 1) = 20
	Grid(7, 1) = 8
	Grid(8, 1) = 20

	Grid(0, 2) = "ÇEK NO"
	Grid(1, 2) = "KEÞÝDECÝ"
	Grid(2, 2) = "BANKA / ÞUBE"
	Grid(3, 2) = "ÞEHÝR"
	Grid(4, 2) = "BANKA ÇEK / HESAP NO"
	Grid(5, 2) = "VADE TARÝHÝ"
	Grid(6, 2) = "TUTARI"
	Grid(7, 2) = ""
	Grid(8, 2) = Doc.DefaultPB & " TUTAR"

	Grid(0, 4) = "C"
	Grid(1, 4) = "C"
	Grid(2, 4) = "C"
	Grid(3, 4) = "C"
	Grid(4, 4) = "C"
	Grid(5, 4) = "D"
	Grid(6, 4) = "N"
	Grid(7, 4) = "C"
	Grid(8, 4) = "N"

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid, 1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i, 0)
		If Grid(i, 4) = "N" Then
			ondalik = MainTableT.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			Grid(i, 6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
	Grid(6, 6) = 2
	Grid(8, 6) = 2
End Sub
Sub SayfaSayisiYazdir(pe1)
	for i = 1 to pe1.GetPageCount()
		res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(sayfaSatirPozr, solMargin + 136, 5, 20, "SAYFA")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, 25, ": " & i & " / " & pe1.GetPageCount)
	Next
End Sub
Function BordroToplamTable()
	AppStr = MainTableT.GetAppName()
	Set C10T = Doc.Select2("XXX", AppStr, "", "", -1)
	C10T.GroupBy2("E_OR_KUR")
	Set BordroToplamTable = C10T
End Function

Sub PrintLabel()
	Set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	Kullanici=Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName)
	
	SayfaBoyu = 82
	SayfaEni = 82
	res = pe1.Open(0, "ETIKET", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "Portrait")
	
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	KutuUsttenMesafe=3
	KutuSoldanMesafe=4 
	
	KutuGenisligi	= 75
	KutuYuksekligi =	75
	
	'res=pe1.SetDevice("SATO_URETIM")
	'sayfa yüksekliðini ayarlar
	'res=pe1.SetDevPaperHeight(SAYFABOYU)
	'sayfa geniþliðini ayarlar
	'res=pe1.SetDevPaperWidth(SAYFAENI)
	'res=pe1.SetDevice("\\fevzi\hp LaserJet 1010 Series Driver") 
	'A5 KAÐIT SEÇÝMÝ
	'res = pe1.SetDevPaperFormat(-14)		
	'res = pe1.PrintDoc(0)
	'Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(0)
	'res=pe1.SetDevice("Oki ML3321")
	'res=pe1.SetDevice("SATO_URETIM")
	'res=pe1.SetDevice("SATO MAMUL")

	For i = 1 To EtiketTablosu.GetRecCount()
		BarkodNo = Trim(EtiketTablosu.SERINO(i))
		
		Qstr = "SELECT * FROM D7KIDSLB"
		Qstr = Qstr &	" WHERE  Trim(KOD) ='"&BarkodNo&"' "
		Set D7LB = Doc.RunSQLQuery("AABBCCDD", Qstr) 
		
		If D7LB.GetRowCount > 0 Then 
			If i <> 1 Then res = pe1.PageBreak
			ETIKETFORMATI = "MAKARA"
			Call Etiket(pe1, BarkodNo)
			
			UygulamaKodu = Trim(D7LB.APPKODU)
			If UygulamaKodu = "SFDC31" Then
				If Trim(D7LB.REFTEXT10) = "E" Then 
					res = pe1.PageBreak
					ETIKETFORMATI = "NUMUNE"
					Call Etiket(pe1, BarkodNo)
				End If
			End If
		End If
	Next
	
	'If D7LB.GetRowCount > 0 Then
	'	Kullanici = Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName)
		'If Kullanici = "URETIM02" Then
		'	If Trim(Doc.GetCurrentForm("N")) <> "SFDC31XXX_ISI_BASLAT" Then
	'			res=pe1.PrintDoc(0)
	'			Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(0)
	'		End If
		'	Else
		'		res = pe1.Show
		'	End If
		'Else
		'	res = pe1.Show
		'End If
		'Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(0)
	'End If

	Kullanici = Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName)
	If Kullanici = "FX" Then
		res = pe1.Show
		Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(0)
	Else
		If D7LB.GetRowCount > 0 Then
			If Kullanici = "URETIM02" Then
				ETIKETFORMATI = ""
				If Trim(Doc.GetCurrentForm("N")) <> "SFDC31XXX_ISI_BASLAT" Then
					res=pe1.PrintDoc(0)
				End If
			Else
				res=pe1.PrintDoc(0)
			End If
			Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(0)
		End If
	End If

End Sub

Sub Etiket(pe1, BarkodNo)
	res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(1)
	
	If ETIKETFORMATI = "NUMUNE" Then
		res = pe1.Box(KutuUsttenMesafe+10, KutuSoldanMesafe, KutuYuksekligi-20, KutuGenisligi)
	Else
		res = pe1.Box(KutuUsttenMesafe, KutuSoldanMesafe, KutuYuksekligi, KutuGenisligi)
	End If
	'res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(0)
	'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	KutuUstu	=	KutuUsttenMesafe
	KutuSolu	= KutuSoldanMesafe
	'-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	StokKodu = Trim(D7LB.SKOD)
	StokAdi = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD", StokKodu, ""))

	Miktar = D7LB.SF_MIKTAR
	Miktar = Doc.NumFormat0(Miktar, 20,2 )
	Birim = Trim(D7LB.SF_UNIT)	
	
	EvrakNo = D7LB.EVRAKNO
	EvratTipi = Trim(D7LB.EVRAKTYPE)
	'StokBirimi = Doc.GetKartValue("STOK00","IUNIT",StokKodu,-1)
	StokGrupKodu1 = Trim(Doc.GetKartValue("STOK00","GK_1",StokKodu, -1))
	StokGrupAdi1 = Trim(Doc.GetGecoValue("STKGK1", "AD", StokGrupKodu1, -1))
	StokGrupKodu2 = Trim(Doc.GetKartValue("STOK00","GK_2",StokKodu,-1))
	StokGrupAdi2 = Trim(Doc.GetGecoValue("STKGK2", "AD", StokGrupKodu2, -1))
	StokGrupKodu3 = Trim(Doc.GetKartValue("STOK00","GK_3",StokKodu,-1))
	StokGrupAdi3 = Trim(Doc.GetGecoValue("STKGK3",

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : HAK6_2014 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : Hurda-01
Hangi Tablo için : STOK29
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için :
