Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satiryuksekligi
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,Tip, satirAra, SatirBitis, bBasla
Public AlanSayisi(), ToplamTutar, ToplamProTutar, n
Public xCizgi()
Public Secilenler()
Public Grid()

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 297
	
	ToplamEn = 0
	ToplamTutar = 0
	ToplamProTutar = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	UstMargin = 15
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 15 
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	satiryuksekligi = 5
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 48
	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 20 * satirYuksekligi
	n = 0

	ReDim xCizgi(1)
	
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satis Siparisi - " & trim(STOK40E.EVRAKNO(1)),  SayfaEni, SayfaBoyu,"LANDSCAPE")
	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	siraNo = 0        
	satirSayisi = STOK40T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
			Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
		
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	Call SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)

	'res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(0)
	res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(255,0,0))
	
	res = pe1.nopen()
	FirmaAdi = Doc.Customp_Gets("FNAMEL0")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.WriteBox(UstMargin-6, SolMargin, 8, 35, "SATIÞ SÝPARÝÞÝ")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin-6, SagMargin-150, 8, 150, Trim(FirmaAdi))
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 11)

	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
	SiparisNo = STOK40E.EVRAKNO(1)
	Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(STOK40E.TARIH(1))

	CHKodu = STOK40E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
	CHAdi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","AD",CHKodu,-1) 
	CHAdi2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","NAME2",CHKodu,-1) 
	Adres1 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",CHKodu,-1) 
	Adres2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",CHKodu,-1) 
	Adres3 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",CHKodu,-1) 
	VergiDairesi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","VERGIDAIRESI",CHKodu,-1) 
	VergiNo = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","VERGINO",CHKodu,-1) 
	Yetkili = STOK40E.SIPIVEREN(1)
	GK1 = STOK40E.GK_1(1)		
	GK2 = STOK40E.GK_2(1)		
	GK3 = STOK40E.GK_3(1)		
	GK1 = Doc.GetGecoValue("ST4GK1","AD",GK1,-1)
	GK2 = Doc.GetGecoValue("ST4GK2","AD",GK2,-1)
	GK3 = Doc.GetGecoValue("ST4GK3","AD",GK3,-1)

	MusteriSipNo = STOK40E.CHSIPNO(1)
	Satici = STOK40E.SIPIALAN(1)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	bBasla = UstMargin + 4
	SatirAra = 5.2
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,cHKodu)
	res = pe1.Write(BBasla + satirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,cHadi)
	satirPozr = BBasla + satirAra
	If trim(cHadi2) <> "" Then
		satirPozr = satirPozr + satirAra
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,cHadi2)
	End If
	If trim(Adres1) <> "" Then
		satirPozr = satirPozr + satirAra
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,Adres1)
	End If
	If trim(Adres2) <> "" Then
		satirPozr = satirPozr + satirAra
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,Adres2)
	End If

	If trim(Adres3) <> "" Then
		satirPozr = satirPozr + satirAra
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,Adres3)
	End If

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,40,"Vergi Dairesi / No")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + SatirAra, SolMargin + 42, 6,2,": ")
	res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + SatirAra, SolMargin + 45, 6,100,trim(VergiDairesi) & " / " & trim(VergiNo))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 185, 6,26, "No")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 215, 6,62,": " & trim(SiparisNo))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(BBasla + SatirAra, SolMargin + 185, 6,26,"Tarih")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(BBasla + SatirAra, SolMargin + 215, 6,62,": " & Tarih)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 185, 6,26,"Satýcý")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 215, 6,62,": " & Satici)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 185, 6,26,"Sipariþi Veren")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 215, 6,62,": " & Yetkili)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 185, 6,26,"Müþteri Sip.No")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 215, 6,62,": " & MusteriSipNo)

	'Sütun baþlýklarý
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	Sol = SolMargin + 2
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		Baslik = Grid(i,2)
		'If i = 3 Or i = 5 Then			
		'	SutunGenisligi = SutunGenisligi + Grid(i+1,1)
		'End If
		res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 11, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 4 , Baslik)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
	Next
	res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
	Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub
'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)

	'res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(0)
	res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(255,0,0))

	Dim satirpozr
	If trim(STOK40T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
	Degisken = ""
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
	If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
		Call Print_form_AraSayfa(pe1)
		siraNo = 1
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic
	Else
		If satirNo = 1 Then
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			satirpozr = SatirBaslangic + siraNo * satiryuksekligi
		End If
		siraNo = siraNo + 1
	End If		
	
	n = n + 1
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Sol = SolMargin + 2
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	
	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
		
		If Left(DegiskenAdi, 1) <> "_" Then
			Degisken = Trim(cstr(STOK40T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi, SatirNo)))
			res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
			If Grid(i,4) = "N" And degiskenadi <> "SF_MIKTAR" And degiskenadi <> "TOPLAMREZERVASYON" Then
				res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
				Ondalik = Grid(i, 6)
				Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18, Ondalik)
			ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
				Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
			ElseIf degiskenadi = "SF_MIKTAR" Or degiskenadi = "TOPLAMREZERVASYON" Then
				res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			ElseIf degiskenadi = "SF_VRI_VR_C" Or degiskenadi = "SF_VRI_VR_R2" Then
				res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
			End If
		End If
		
		res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 3, Degisken)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
	Next					 
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub
'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Print_form_AraSayfa(pe1)
	'dýþ çerçeve
	SatirBitis = SatirSonu
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.8)
	res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(1)
	res = pe1.box(SatirBaslangic-10, SolMargin, satirBitis-SatirBaslangic+10, SagMargin-SolMargin)

	'dikey çizgiler
	cizgiBoyu = SatirBitis - UstMargin - 47.5
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	'yatay çizgi
	res = pe1.pensize(0.5)
	res = pe1.Line(SatirBaslangic - 2, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin-SolMargin)		

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) 
		'If i <> 3 And i <> 5 Then
			LineX = xCizgi(i)		
			res = pe1.Line(UstMargin+47.5, LineX, cizgiBoyu, 0)
		'End If
	Next
End Sub
'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	'dýþ çerçeve
	SatirBitis = SatirSonu
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.8)
	res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(1)
	res = pe1.box(satirBaslangic-12, SolMargin, satirBitis-satirBaslangic+12, SagMargin-SolMargin)

	'dikey çizgiler
	cizgiBoyu = SatirBitis - satirBaslangic + 12
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		'If i <> 3 And i <> 5 Then
			LineX = xCizgi(i)		
			res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 12, LineX, cizgiBoyu, 0)
		'End If
	Next
	
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.pensize(0.5)
	res = pe1.Line(SatirBaslangic-2, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin-SolMargin)		

	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)

	Odeme1 = STOK40E.ODEMEKOSULLARI_1(1)
	Odeme2 = STOK40E.ODEMEKOSULLARI_2(1)
	Not1 = STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1(1)
	Not2 = STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2(1)
	sevkAdresi1 = STOK40E.SEVKADRESI_1(1)
	sevkAdresi2 = STOK40E.SEVKADRESI_2(1)
	sevkAdresi3 = STOK40E.SEVKADRESI_3(1)

	Blok2 = SolMargin + 35
	Genislik = xCizgi(4) - Blok2 - 2		
	satirpozr = SatirBitis + 2

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)

	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Solmargin+2, 5, 30, "Ödeme")
	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Solmargin+32, 5, 3,": " )
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Blok2, 5, Genislik, Trim(Odeme1))

	If Trim(odeme2)<> "" Then
		satirpozr = SatirPozr + SatirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Blok2, 5, Genislik, Trim(Odeme2))
	End If		

	satirpozr = satirpozr + SatirYuksekligi	
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Solmargin + 2, 5,30,"Notlar")
	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Solmargin + 32, 5,3,":")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)

	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Blok2,6,Genislik, Not1)

	If Trim(Not2)<> "" Then  
		satirpozr = satirpozr + SatirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Blok2,6,Genislik, Not2)
	End If			

	satirpozr = satirpozr + SatirYuksekligi
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Solmargin + 2, 5,30,"Sevk Adresi")
	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin + 32, 5,3,": " )
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Blok2,6,Genislik, sevkAdresi1)

	If Trim(sevkAdresi2) <> "" Then  
		satirpozr = satirpozr + SatirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Blok2,6,Genislik, sevkAdresi2)
	End If
	
	If Trim(sevkAdresi3) <> "" Then  
		satirpozr = satirpozr + SatirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Blok2,6,Genislik, sevkAdresi3)
	End If
		
	Alt = pe1.GetBottom+2

	Ondalik = Grid(7,6)
	ToplamTutar = STOK40E.OR_NEWTOPLAM(1)
	IskontoOrani = STOK40E.DO_ISKONTOX_ORANI(1)
	IskontoToplami=STOK40E.OR_ISKONTOLARTOPLAMI(1)
	NetTutar = STOK40E.OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM(1)
	KDVTutari = STOK40E.OR_KDVLERTOPLAM(1)
	KDVDahilTutar = STOK40E.OR_KDVDAHILTOPLAM(1)
	KDVOrani = STOK40E.DO_KDV_ORANI(1)

	ToplamTutar = Doc.NumFormat(ToplamTutar,18,Ondalik)
	IskontoOrani = Doc.NumFormat(IskontoOrani,18,Ondalik)
	IskontoToplami = Doc.NumFormat(IskontoToplami,18,Ondalik)
	NetTutar = Doc.NumFormat(NetTutar,18,Ondalik)
	KDVOrani = Doc.NumFormat(KDVOrani,18,Ondalik)
	KDVTutari = Doc.NumFormat(KDVTutari,18,Ondalik)
	KDVDahilTutar = Doc.NumFormat(KDVDahilTutar,18,Ondalik)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	sss = SatirYuksekligi

	SatirYuksekligi = 6

	'res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2,xCizgi(4) + 3, 6, 30,"TOPLAM")
	'res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2 + satirYuksekligi,xCizgi(4) + 3,6,40,"ÝSKONTO %  " & IskontoOrani)
	'res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2 + 2 * satirYuksekligi,xCizgi(4) + 3,6,30,"NET TOPLAM")
	'res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2 + 3 * satirYuksekligi,xCizgi(4) + 3,6,40,"KDV %  " & KDVOrani)
	'res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	'res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	'res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 3.5 + 4 * satirYuksekligi,xCizgi(4) + 3,6,30,"GENEL TOPLAM")

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)

	lll = xCizgi(8)
	xCizgi(8) = xCizgi(7)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	Gen = xCizgi(8) - xCizgi(6) - 4
	'res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2,xCizgi(6) + 2,6,Gen, ToplamTutar)
	'res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2 + satirYuksekligi,xCizgi(6) + 2,6,Gen, IskontoToplami)
	'res = pe1.Line(SatirBitis + 2 + 2 * satirYuksekligi - 1,xCizgi(6),0,xCizgi(8) - xCizgi(6))
	'res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	'res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 3.5 + 2 * satirYuksekligi,xCizgi(6) + 2,6,Gen, NetTutar)
	'res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2 + 3 * satirYuksekligi,xCizgi(6) + 2,12,Gen, KDVTutari)
	'res = pe1.Line(SatirBitis + 2 + 4 * satirYuksekligi - 1,xCizgi(6),0,xCizgi(8) - xCizgi(6))
	'res = pe1.Line(SatirBitis + 2 + 4 * satirYuksekligi,xCizgi(6),0,xCizgi(8) - xCizgi(6))
	'res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	'res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 3.5 + 4 * satirYuksekligi,xCizgi(6) + 2,6,Gen, KDVDahilTutar)
	xCizgi(8) = lll
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

End Sub
'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	If pe1.GetPageCount > 1 Then
		For i = 1 To pe1.GetPageCount
			res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
			res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 6 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 185, 6,26,"Sayfa")
			res = pe1.SetBold(0)
			res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 6 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 215, 6,50,": " & i & " / " & pe1.GetPageCount)
		Next
	End If
End Sub
'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(9,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2,0) = "SF_VRI_VR_C"
	Grid(3,0) = "NOTES"
	Grid(4,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(5,0) = "SF_VRI_VR_R2"
	Grid(6,0) = "SF_VRI_VR_R1"
	Grid(7,0) = "RTESTARIH"
	Grid(8,0) = "REV_RTESTARIH"
	
	Grid(0,1) = 42
	Grid(1,1) = 55
	Grid(2,1) = 16
	Grid(3,1) = 59
	Grid(4,1) = 22
	Grid(5,1) = 18
	Grid(6,1) = 25
	Grid(7,1) = 20
	Grid(8,1) = 20

	PB = Trim(STOK40T.PRICEUNIT(1))
	If PB = "ECU" Then PB = "EURO"

	Grid(0,2) = "Stok Kodu"
	Grid(1,2) = "Açýklama"
	Grid(2,2) = "Büküm Yönü"
	Grid(3,2) = "Notlar"
	Grid(4,2) = "Miktar"
	Grid(5,2) = "L1-L2-F"
	Grid(6,2) = "Makara"
	Grid(7,2) = "Ýstenilen T. Tarihi"
	Grid(8,2) = "Verilen T. Tarihi"

	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "C"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	Grid(5,4) = "C"
	Grid(6,4) = "C"
	Grid(7,4) = "D"
	Grid(8,4) = "D"

	Sol = SolMargin
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i,0)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" And Left(degisken,1) <> "_" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = STOK40T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			If Ondalik > 2 Then Ondalik = 2
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
		'If grid(i,0) = "_IFIYAT" Then grid(i,6) = 2
		SutunGenisligi = grid(i,1)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)-1) = Sol
		ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
	Next	
	For i = 0 To uBound(Grid,1) - 1
		ToplamEn = ToplamEn + Grid(i,1)
	Next
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.setitalic(0)
		res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
		res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
		res = pe1.setrotation(0)
		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
		res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
End Sub

Kod : 
Public ustmargin, solmargin, sagMargin, satirYuksekligi, sirano, MainTableE, MainTableT		
Dim sayfaSatirsayisi, AraToplam, Grid, satirSonu, satirBaslangic
Dim solTutar, GenTutar, solPB, GenPB, xCizgi(), sayfaSatirPozr, solTLTutar, GenTLTutar
Sub PrintForm()
	If not IsObject(CKSN22E) Then
		Set MainTableE = Doc.GetTableObject("CKSN10E")
		Set MainTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("CKSN10T")
	Else
		Set MainTableE = Doc.GetTableObject("CKSN22E")
		Set MainTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("CKSN22T")
	End if

	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210

	Set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Fatura", sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")

	satirYuksekligi = 4.3

	Redim xCizgi(0)

	Ustmargin = 2 * satirYuksekligi
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 17 * satirYuksekligi

	solmargin = 6
	sagMargin = SayfaEni - 6
	satirpozr = 0

	sayfaSatirsayisi = 30
	sirano = 0
	sayfaSatirPozr = UstMargin + 9 * satirYuksekligi
	MainTableT.SafePack

	satirSayisi = MainTableT.GetRecCount
	Call Alanlar()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	Call PrintFormSayfaSonu(pe1)
	Call SayfaSayisiYazdir(pe1)
	
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	satirPozr = 63
	SatirSonu = 260
	SonSatir = 0
	Sutuny = 63
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Firma = Doc.Customp_GetS("FNAMEL0")
	res = pe1.Setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin, solMargin, 5, sagMargin - solMargin, trim(Firma))
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 6, solMargin, 5, sagMargin - solMargin, "Ç E K   Ç I K I Þ   B O R D R O S U")

	res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.SetItalic(0)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.PenColor(RGB(180, 180, 180))

	CHKodu = MainTableE.CIKISCODE
	Unvan1 = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", CHKodu, "")
	Unvan2 = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00", "NAME2", CHKodu, "")
	Adres1 = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00", "ADRES_1", CHKodu, "")
	Adres2 = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00", "ADRES_2", CHKodu, "")
	Adres3 = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00", "ADRES_3", CHKodu, "")
	VergiDairesi = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("CARI00", "VERGIDAIRESI", CHKodu, "")
	VergiNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "VERGINO", CHKodu, "")

	BBasla = 30
	KutuEn = 100
	res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 1, 5, KutuEn, CHKodu)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, KutuEn, Unvan1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, KutuEn, Unvan2)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, KutuEn, Adres1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, KutuEn, Adres2)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, KutuEn, Adres3)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, KutuEn, "Vergi Dairesi : " & VergiDairesi & " Vergi No : " & VergiNo)

	res = pe1.Box(BBasla - 2, solMargin, pe1.GetBottom - BBasla + 2, KutuEn + 2)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(sayfaSatirPozr + satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 136, 10, 20, "TARÝH")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, 25, ": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(MainTableE.TARIH))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 136, 10, 20, "BORDRO NO")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, 25, ": " & trim(MainTableE.EVRAKNO))

	Sol = SolMargin + 1
	res = pe1.Setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid, 1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i, 1)
		Baslik = Grid(i, 2)
		res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 3 * satirYuksekligi, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 2 , Baslik)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		xCizgi(uBound(XCizgi)) = sol - 1
		Redim Preserve xCizgi(uBound(XCizgi) + 1)
	Next
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
	If trim(MainTableT.E_CKSNNO(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
	Degisken = ""
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
	If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
		Call PrintFormSayfaSonu(pe1)
		siraNo = 1
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic
	Else
		If satirNo = 1 Then
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			satirpozr = SatirBaslangic + siraNo * satiryuksekligi
		End If
		siraNo = siraNo + 1
	End If		
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Sol = SolMargin + 1
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid, 1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i, 1) 
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i, 0)				
		if degiskenAdi = "_BANKASUBE" then
			Degisken = trim(MainTableT.E_BANKA(satirNo)) & " / " & trim(MainTableT.E_SUBE(satirNo))
		Elseif degiskenAdi = "_SEHIR" then
			Degisken = Doc.GetGecoValue("SEHIR","AD",MainTableT.E_SEHIR(satirNo),-1)
		Elseif degiskenAdi = "_CEKHESAPNO" then
			Degisken = trim(MainTableT.E_OREVRAKNO(satirNo)) & " / " & trim(MainTableT.E_HESAPNO(satirNo))
		else
			Degisken = MainTableT.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi, SatirNo)
		end if
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		if degiskenAdi = "E_OR_TUTAR" then
			solTutar = sol
			GenTutar = SutunGenisligi
		Elseif degiskenAdi = "E_TL_TUTAR" then
			solTLTutar = sol
			GenTLTutar = SutunGenisligi
		Elseif degiskenAdi = "E_OR_KUR" then
			solPB = sol
			GenPB = SutunGenisligi
		end if
		If Grid(i, 4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i, 6)
			Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18, Ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i, 4) = "D" Then
			Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
		End If		
		res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 2, Degisken)			
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
	Next					 
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub
Sub PrintFormSayfaSonu(pe1)
	satirUst = satirBaslangic -  4 * satirYuksekligi
	satirSonu = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi
'dýþ çerçeve
	res = pe1.PenColor(RGB(180, 180, 180))
	res = pe1.box(satirUst, SolMargin, satirSonu - satirUst, SagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - satirYuksekligi, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)

'dikey çizgiler
	For i = 0 To UBound(xCizgi) - 2
		LineX = cint(xCizgi(i))
		If lineX = 0 Then Exit For
		if i <> 6 then
			res = pe1.Line(satirUst, LineX, satirSonu - satirUst, 0)
		end if
	Next

	sBAsla = satirSonu + satirYuksekligi
	res = pe1.line(sBAsla, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	ToplamTutar = MainTableE.TL_TOPLAMTUTAR

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 1, solMargin + 1, 5, 30, "TOPLAM ADET")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, 30, ": "& MainTableT.GetRecCount)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 1, 5, 30, "BORDRO TOPLAMI")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 8, 2, ":")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	ToplamBasla = pe1.GetTop
	Set ToplamTable = BordroToplamTable()
	if ToplamTable.GetRecCount = 1 Then
	 	res = pe1.Write(ToplamBasla, solTutar, 5, GenTutar - 2, Doc.NumFormat(ToplamTable.E_OR_TUTAR(1),20,Grid(6,6)))
	 	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solPB, 5, GenPB - 2, ToplamTable.E_OR_KUR(1))
	Else
		ToplamBasla = ToplamBasla - satirYuksekligi
		for i = 1 to ToplamTable.GetRecCount
		 	res = pe1.Write(ToplamBasla + (i-1) * satirYuksekligi, solTutar, 5, GenTutar - 2, Doc.NumFormat(ToplamTable.E_OR_TUTAR(i),20,Grid(6,6)))
		 	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solPB, 5, GenPB - 2, ToplamTable.E_OR_KUR(i))
		Next
	End if
 	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solTLTutar, 5, GenTLTutar - 2, Doc.NumFormat(ToplamTutar,20,Grid(8,6)))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	PB = Doc.DefaultPB
' 	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solPB, 5, 20, Doc.DefaultPB)

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 1, 5, 30, "ORTALAMA VADE")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, 25, ": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(MainTableE.ORTALAMARVALOR))
	
	
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 1, 5, 30, "AÇIKLAMA")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, 45, ": " & (MainTableE.NOTES))
	

	res = pe1.line(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)

	Yalniz = Doc.NumToText_TL(ToplamTutar)
	if trim(PB) = trim(Doc.DefaultPB) then
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 1, 5, sagMargin - solMargin, "YALNIZ " & Yalniz & " hesabýnýza borç kaydedilmiþtir.")
	end if

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 1, 5, 52, "YETKÝLÝ")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 75, 5, 52, "DÜZENLEYEN")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 139, 5, 52, "TESLÝM ALAN")
	alt = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4 * satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 1, 5, 52, "LÜTFEN FAKSLAYINIZ")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solmargin + 139, 5, 52, "ÖDEMEYÝ EKSÝKSÝZ")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 52, "TESLÝM ALDIM")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 52, "KAÞE - ÝMZA")

	res = pe1.PenColor(RGB(180, 180, 180))

	res = pe1.line(alt, solMargin + 1, 0, 40)
	res = pe1.line(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 75, 0, 40)
	res = pe1.line(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 139, 0, 40)
End Sub
Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(8, 7)

	Grid(0, 0) = "E_CKSNNO"
	Grid(1, 0) = "E_KESIDECI"
	Grid(2, 0) = "_BANKASUBE"
	Grid(3, 0) = "_SEHIR"
	Grid(4, 0) = "_CEKHESAPNO"
	Grid(5, 0) = "E_RVADE"
	Grid(6, 0) = "E_OR_TUTAR"
	Grid(7, 0) = "E_OR_KUR"
	Grid(8, 0) = "E_TL_TUTAR"

	Grid(0, 1) = 12
	Grid(1, 1) = 37
	Grid(2, 1) = 34
	Grid(3, 1) = 19
	Grid(4, 1) = 24
	Grid(5, 1) = 17
	Grid(6, 1) = 26
	Grid(7, 1) = 8
	Grid(8, 1) = 20

	Grid(0, 2) = "ÇEK NO"
	Grid(1, 2) = "KEÞÝDECÝ"
	Grid(2, 2) = "BANKA / ÞUBE"
	Grid(3, 2) = "ÞEHÝR"
	Grid(4, 2) = "BANKA ÇEK / HESAP NO"
	Grid(5, 2) = "VADE TARÝHÝ"
	Grid(6, 2) = "TUTARI"
	Grid(7, 2) = ""
	Grid(8, 2) = Doc.DefaultPB & " TUTAR"

	Grid(0, 4) = "C"
	Grid(1, 4) = "C"
	Grid(2, 4) = "C"
	Grid(3, 4) = "C"
	Grid(4, 4) = "C"
	Grid(5, 4) = "D"
	Grid(6, 4) = "N"
	Grid(7, 4) = "C"
	Grid(8, 4) = "N"

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid, 1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i, 0)
		If Grid(i, 4) = "N" Then
			ondalik = MainTableT.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			Grid(i, 6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
	Grid(6, 6) = 2
	Grid(8, 6) = 2
End Sub
Sub SayfaSayisiYazdir(pe1)
	for i = 1 to pe1.GetPageCount()
		res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(sayfaSatirPozr, solMargin + 136, 5, 20, "SAYFA")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, 25, ": " & i & " / " & pe1.GetPageCount)
	Next
End Sub
Function BordroToplamTable()
	AppStr = MainTableT.GetAppName()
	Set C10T = Doc.Select2("XXX", AppStr, "", "", -1)
	C10T.GroupBy2("E_OR_KUR")
	Set BordroToplamTable = C10T
End Function

Public ustmargin, solmargin, sagMargin, satirYuksekligi, sirano, MainTableE, MainTableT
Dim sayfaSatirsayisi, AraToplam, Grid, satirSonu, satirBaslangic
Dim solTutar, GenTutar, solPB, GenPB, xCizgi(), sayfaSatirPozr
Sub PrintForm()
	If not IsObject(CKSN21E) Then
		Set MainTableE = Doc.GetTableObject("CKSN10E")
		Set MainTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("CKSN10T")
	Else
		Set MainTableE = Doc.GetTableObject("CKSN21E")
		Set MainTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("CKSN21T")
	End if
		
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210

	Set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Fatura", sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")

	satirYuksekligi = 4.3

	Redim xCizgi(0)

	Ustmargin = 2 * satirYuksekligi
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 17 * satirYuksekligi

	solmargin = 6
	sagMargin = SayfaEni - 6
	satirpozr = 0

	sayfaSatirsayisi = 30
	sirano = 0
	sayfaSatirPozr = UstMargin + 9 * satirYuksekligi
	MainTableT.SafePack

	satirSayisi = MainTableT.GetRecCount
	Call Alanlar()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	Call PrintFormSayfaSonu(pe1)
	Call SayfaSayisiYazdir(pe1)
	
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	satirPozr = 63
	SatirSonu = 260
	SonSatir = 0
	Sutuny = 63
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Firma = Doc.Customp_GetS("FNAMEL0")
	res = pe1.Setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin, solMargin, 5, sagMargin - solMargin, trim(Firma))
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 6, solMargin, 5, sagMargin - solMargin, "Ç E K   T R A N S F E R   B O R D R O S U")

	res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.SetItalic(0)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.PenColor(RGB(180, 180, 180))

	GirisPortfoyKodu = MainTableE.GIRISCODE
	GirisPortfoyAdi = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("GDEF01","AD", GirisPortfoyKodu,"")

	CikisPortfoyKodu = MainTableE.CIKISCODE
	CikisPortfoyAdi = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("GDEF01","AD", CikisPortfoyKodu,"")

	BBasla = 30
	KutuEn = 100
	res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 1, 5, 30, "Hangi Portföyden")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, KutuEn - pe1.GetRight, GirisPortfoyKodu)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, KutuEn - pe1.GetLeft, GirisPortfoyAdi)

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 3 * satirYuksekligi, SolMargin + 1, 5, 30, "Hangi Portföye")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, KutuEn - pe1.GetRight, CikisPortfoyKodu)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop  + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, KutuEn - pe1.GetLeft, CikisPortfoyAdi)

	res = pe1.Box(BBasla - satirYuksekligi, solMargin, pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirYuksekligi - BBasla, KutuEn + 2)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(sayfaSatirPozr + satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 136, 10, 20, "TARÝH")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, 25, ": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(MainTableE.TARIH))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 136, 10, 20, "BORDRO NO")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, 25, ": " & trim(MainTableE.EVRAKNO))

	Sol = SolMargin + 1
	res = pe1.Setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid, 1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i, 1)
		Baslik = Grid(i, 2)
		res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 3 * satirYuksekligi, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 2 , Baslik)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		xCizgi(uBound(XCizgi)) = sol - 1
		Redim Preserve xCizgi(uBound(XCizgi) + 1)
	Next
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
	If trim(MainTableT.E_CKSNNO(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
	Degisken = ""
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
	If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
		Call PrintFormSayfaSonu(pe1)
		siraNo = 1
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic
	Else
		If satirNo = 1 Then
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			satirpozr = SatirBaslangic + siraNo * satiryuksekligi
		End If
		siraNo = siraNo + 1
	End If		
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Sol = SolMargin + 1
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid, 1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i, 1) 
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i, 0)				
		if degiskenAdi = "_BANKASUBE" then
			Degisken = trim(MainTableT.E_BANKA(satirNo)) & " / " & trim(MainTableT.E_SUBE(satirNo))
		Elseif degiskenAdi = "_SEHIR" then
			Degisken = Doc.GetGecoValue("SEHIR","AD",MainTableT.E_SEHIR(satirNo),-1)
		Elseif degiskenAdi = "_CEKHESAPNO" then
			Degisken = trim(MainTableT.E_OREVRAKNO(satirNo)) & " / " & trim(MainTableT.E_HESAPNO(satirNo))
		else
			Degisken = MainTableT.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi, SatirNo)
		end if
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		If Grid(i, 4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i, 6)
			Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18, Ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i, 4) = "D" Then
			Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
		End If		
		if degiskenAdi = "E_OR_TUTAR" then
			solTutar = sol
			GenTutar = SutunGenisligi
		Elseif degiskenAdi = "E_OR_KUR" then
			solPB = sol
			GenPB = SutunGenisligi
		end if
		res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 2, Degisken)			
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
	Next					 
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub
Sub PrintFormSayfaSonu(pe1)
	satirUst = satirBaslangic -  4 * satirYuksekligi
	satirSonu = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi
'dýþ çerçeve
	res = pe1.PenColor(RGB(180, 180, 180))
	res = pe1.box(satirUst, SolMargin, satirSonu - satirUst, SagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - satirYuksekligi, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)

'dikey çizgiler
	For i = 0 To UBound(xCizgi) - 2
		LineX = cint(xCizgi(i))
		If lineX = 0 Then Exit For
		if i <> 6 then
			res = pe1.Line(satirUst, LineX, satirSonu - satirUst, 0)
		end if
	Next

	sBAsla = satirSonu + satirYuksekligi
	res = pe1.line(sBAsla, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	ToplamTutar = MainTableE.TL_TOPLAMTUTAR
	OrjinalTutar = MainTableE.PB_1_OR_TUTAR
	OrjinalKur = MainTableE.PB_1

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 1, solMargin + 1, 5, 30, "TOPLAM ADET")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, 30, ": "& MainTableT.GetRecCount)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 1, 5, 30, "BORDRO TOPLAMI")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 8, 2, ":")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 150, 5, GenTutar - 2,Doc.NumFormat(OrjinalTutar,20,Grid(6,6))&" "&OrjinalKur)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 175, 5, GenTutar - 2, Doc.NumFormat(ToplamTutar,20,Grid(8,6))&" "&"TL")
 	'res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, GenTutar - 2, Doc.NumFormat(ToplamTutar,20,Grid(6,6)))
 	
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	PB = Doc.DefaultPB
 	'res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solPB, 5, 20, Doc.DefaultPB)
 	
 	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 1, 5, 30, "ORTALAMA YAÞ")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, 25, ": " & (MainTableE.ADAT))

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 1, 5, 30, "ORTALAMA VADE")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, 25, ": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(MainTableE.ORTALAMARVALOR))
	
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 1, 5, 45, "VADE FARKI ORANI ve TUTARI")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, 45, ": " & "% " & (MainTableE.VALORORANI) & "  -  " & Doc.NumFormat((MainTableE.HVF),24,3) & " TL")
	
	
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 1, 5, 30, "AÇIKLAMA")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, 45, ": " & (MainTableE.NOTES))
	

	res = pe1.line(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)

	Yalniz = Doc.NumToText_TL(ToplamTutar)
	if trim(PB) = trim(Doc.DefaultPB) then
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 1, 5, sagMargin - solMargin, "YALNIZ " & Yalniz)
	end if

'	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 1, 5, 52, "YETKÝLÝ")
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 75, 5, 52, "DÜZENLEYEN")
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 139, 5, 52, "TESLÝM ALAN")
'	alt = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi

'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4 * satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 1, 5, 52, "LÜTFEN FAKSLAYINIZ")
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solmargin + 139, 5, 52, "ÖDEMEYÝ EKSÝKSÝZ")
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 52, "TESLÝM ALDIM")
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 52, "KAÞE - ÝMZA")

'	res = pe1.PenColor(RGB(180, 180, 180))

'	res = pe1.line(alt, solMargin + 1, 0, 40)

End Sub
Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(8, 7)

	Grid(0, 0) = "E_CKSNNO"
	Grid(1, 0) = "E_KESIDECI"
	Grid(2, 0) = "_BANKASUBE"
	Grid(3, 0) = "_SEHIR"
	Grid(4, 0) = "_CEKHESAPNO"
	Grid(5, 0) = "E_RVADE"
	Grid(6, 0) = "E_OR_TUTAR"
	Grid(7, 0) = "E_OR_KUR"
	Grid(8, 0) = "E_TL_TUTAR"

	Grid(0, 1) = 12
	Grid(1, 1) = 34
	Grid(2, 1) = 35
	Grid(3, 1) = 21
	Grid(4, 1) = 24
	Grid(5, 1) = 17
	Grid(6, 1) = 25
	Grid(7, 1) = 7
	Grid(8, 1) = 22

	Grid(0, 2) = "ÇEK NO"
	Grid(1, 2) = "KEÞÝDECÝ"
	Grid(2, 2) = "BANKA / ÞUBE"
	Grid(3, 2) = "ÞEHÝR"
	Grid(4, 2) = "BANKA ÇEK / HESAP NO"
	Grid(5, 2) = "VADE TARÝHÝ"
	Grid(6, 2) = "TUTARI"
	Grid(7, 2) = ""
	Grid(8, 2) = " TL TUTAR"
	'Grid(8, 2) = Doc.DefaultPB & " TUTAR"

	Grid(0, 4) = "C"
	Grid(1, 4) = "C"
	Grid(2, 4) = "C"
	Grid(3, 4) = "C"
	Grid(4, 4) = "C"
	Grid(5, 4) = "D"
	Grid(6, 4) = "N"
	Grid(7, 4) = "C"
	Grid(8, 4) = "N"

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid, 1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i, 0)
		If Grid(i, 4) = "N" Then
			ondalik = MainTableT.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			Grid(i, 6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
	Grid(6, 6) = 2
	Grid(8, 6) = 2
End Sub
Sub SayfaSayisiYazdir(pe1)
	for i = 1 to pe1.GetPageCount()
		res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(sayfaSatirPozr, solMargin + 136, 5, 20, "SAYFA")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, 25, ": " & i & " / " & pe1.GetPageCount)
	Next
End Sub

Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan, TopBrut, TopNet, TopDara
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,Tip
Public AlanSayisi()		 
Public xCizgi()
Public Secilenler()
Public Grid
Sub PrintForm()
	ReDim AlanSayisi(100)		 
	ReDim xCizgi(100)
	ReDim Secilenler(100,3)
	ToplamEn = 0
	BasilabilirAlan = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	ToplamEn = 210
	TopBrut = 0
	TopNet = 0
	TopDara = 0

	'sayfaTuru = InputBox("Sayfa Türünü Giriniz" & Chr(13) & "A - A4, L - Letter (Sürekli Form)", "Dinamo", "A")
	sayfaTuru = "A"
	Select Case UCase(sayfaTuru)
		Case "L"
			sayfaBoyu = 279.4
			sayfaEni = 215.9
		Case "A"
			sayfaBoyu = 297
			sayfaEni = 210
		Case Else
			K = MsgBox("A veya L giriniz", 48, "Uyarý")
			Exit Sub
	End Select					
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	Call Alanlar()
	
	If ToplamEn > 1.1 * SayfaEni Then
		k = SayfaBoyu
		l = SayfaEni
		SayfaEni = k
		SayfaBoyu = l
		Tip = "LANDSCAPE"
		res = pe1.Open(0, "TEST SERTÝFÝKASI", SayfaEni, SayfaBoyu, Tip)
	Else
		Tip = ""
		res = pe1.Open(0, "TEST SERTÝFÝKASI", SayfaBoyu,SayfaEni , Tip)
	End If		

	BasilabilirAlan = SayfaEni - 20
	satirSonu = sayfaBoyu - 20

	satirBaslangic = 80

	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	Dim satirSayisi_tableT
	satirSayisi_tableT = STOK60T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)

	For I = 1 To satirSayisi_tableT
    if (mid(trim(STOK60T.KOD(i)),1,5)) <>"31 30" then
		   Call Print_form_satir(pe1, I)
		end if
	Next
	
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", "9")	
	res = pe1.setalign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(48 ,  165,  6, 18, Doc.NumFormat(TopBrut,15,1))
	res = pe1.Write(54 ,  165,  6, 18, Doc.NumFormat(TopNet,15,1))
	res = pe1.Write(60 ,  165,  6, 18, Doc.NumFormat(TopDara,15,1))
	
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(1001)
	res = pe1.nopen()
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(0)
	res = pe1.box(8, 8, 30, SayfaEni-110)
	res = pe1.UseSet(1001)
	res = pe1.RemoveSet(1001)
        
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)

	res = pe1.nopen()
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
	If Tip = "LANDSCAPE" Then
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 16)
		res = pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
		res = pe1.write(15, 10, 15, SayfaEni - 30, "TEST SERTÝFÝKASI")
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		If logo <> "" Then
			res = pe1.Picture(12, 15, 15, 55, logo, 18)
		End If
	Else
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 16)
		res = pe1.setbold(1)
		res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
		res = pe1.write(18, 10, 15, SayfaEni - 30, "TEST SERTÝFÝKASI")
		res = pe1.setalign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
		res = pe1.write(12, 10, 15, SayfaEni - 30, "HAK BAKIR ÇEKME ve ")
		res = pe1.write(18, 10, 15, SayfaEni - 30, "ELEKTROTEL SAN.TÝC.AÞ.")
		'res = pe1.write(18, 10, 15, SayfaEni - 30, "TEST SERTÝFÝKASI")
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		If logo <> "" Then
			res = pe1.Picture(11, 15, 10, 35, logo, 18)
		End If
	End If
	res = pe1.selectfont("Helvetica", "12")
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", "10")

	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
'*******************
	BBasla = 30
	SatirAra = 6

	FisNo = STOK60E.EVRAKNO(1)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla , 12, 6, 25, "Ýrsaliye No")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla , 38, 6, 25, ": " & trim(FisNo))

	'AmbarKodu = STOK60E.AMBCODE(1)

	'res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	'res = pe1.write(BBasla , 96, 6, 50, ": " & trim(AmbarKodu) & " - " & trim(AmbarAdi))

	Tarih = doc.StrtoNorDate(STOK60E.TARIH(1))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla , 156, 6, 25, "Tarih")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla , 172, 6, 20, ": " & trim(Tarih))

	'MusteriKodu = STOK60E.CH_KODU(1)
	MusteriAdi = STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_AD(1)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla + satirAra , 12, 6, 25, "Müþteri")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla + satirAra , 38, 6, SayfaEni - 75, ": " &  trim(MusteriAdi))

	'Sevktarihi = doc.StrtoNorDate(STOK60E.RSEVKTARIH(1))
	'res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	'res = pe1.write(BBasla + 2 * satirAra , 12, 6, 25, "Sevk Tarihi")
	'res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	'res = pe1.write(BBasla + 2 * satirAra , 38, 6, 20, ": " & trim(Sevktarihi))

	MatbuIrsNo = STOK60E.EXTERNALEVRAKNO(1)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	'res = pe1.write(BBasla + 2 * satirAra , 70, 6, 25, "Matbu Ýrs. No")
	res = pe1.write(BBasla + 3 * satirAra , 12, 6, 25, "Matbu Ýrs. No")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	'res = pe1.write(BBasla + 2 * satirAra , 96, 6, 25, ": " & trim(MatbuIrsNo))
	res = pe1.write(BBasla + 3 * satirAra , 40, 6, 25, ": " & trim(MatbuIrsNo))

	'If trim(STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_1(1)) = "" Then
	'	SevkAdres1 = STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_1(1)
		'SevkAdres2 = STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_2(1)
		'SevkAdres3 = STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_3(1)
	'Else
	'	SevkAdres1 = STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_1(1)
		'SevkAdres2 = STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_2(1)
		'SevkAdres3 = STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_3(1)
	'End If
	'res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	'res = pe1.write(BBasla + 3 * satirAra , 12, 6, 25, "Adres")
	'res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	'res = pe1.write(BBasla + 3 * satirAra , 38, 6, 100, ": " & trim(SevkAdres1))
	'res = pe1.write(BBasla + 4 * satirAra , 38, 6, 100, ": " & trim(SevkAdres2))
	'res = pe1.write(BBasla + 5 * satirAra , 38, 6, 100, ": " & trim(SevkAdres3))		


	'NakliyeSirketi = STOK60E.BOX1_1(1)
	'Sofor = STOK60E.BOX1_2(1)
	'PlakaNo = STOK60E.BOX1_3(1)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", "9")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla + 3 * satirAra , 140, 6, 40, "Toplam Brüt (Kg)"&":")
	res = pe1.write(BBasla + 4 * satirAra , 140, 6, 40, "Toplam Net (Kg)"&":")
	res = pe1.write(BBasla + 5 * satirAra , 140, 6, 40, "Toplam Dara (Kg)"&":")		
	'res = pe1.SetBold(0)		
	'res = pe1.write(BBasla + 3 * satirAra , 166, 6, 33, ": " & trim(NakliyeSirketi))
	'res = pe1.write(BBasla + 4 * satirAra , 166, 6, 33, ": " & trim(Sofor))
	'res = pe1.write(BBasla + 5 * satirAra , 166, 6, 33, ": " & trim(PlakaNo))		
	
	
	
	
	
	
	
	
'***********************		
	Oran = BasilabilirAlan / ToplamEn

	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	'FontBoyu = FontAyarla(BasilabilirAlan,ToplamEn,10)
	'res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", FontBoyu)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 7)
	Sol = 12
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		'SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) / 3.22 * Oran
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
		Baslik = Grid(i,2)
		If SutunGenisligi > 0 Then	
			res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 13, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 4 , Baslik)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		xCizgi(i) =	Sol -2
		End If
	Next

 	res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
'yatay çizgiler
	res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
	res = pe1.Line(28, 10, 0, SayfaEni-20)
	res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 14, 10, 0, sayfaEni -20)
	res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 2, 10, 0, sayfaEni -20)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(1001)
	res = pe1.PenColor(RGB(0, 0, 0))        
	res = pe1.pensize(0.5)

	Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr, satiryuksekligi
	satiryuksekligi = 6
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
	If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		siraNo = 1
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic
	Else
		If satirNo = 1 Then
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
		End If
		siraNo = siraNo + 1
	End If
		
	res = pe1.StoreSet(1001)
	FontBoyu = FontAyarla(BasilabilirAlan,ToplamEn,10)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", FontBoyu)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Sol = 12
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	


	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		'SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)  / 3.22 * BasilabilirAlan / ToplamEn
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)  
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
		Degisken = trim(cstr(STOK60T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))
		
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 8)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	
		
		'If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
		'	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		'	Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
		'	Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,Ondalik)
		'ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
		'	Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
		'End If
		
		If SutunGenisligi > 0   then
			res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 4, Degisken)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			
		End If	
		
		
	Next	
	
		
		Set S01 = Doc.Select1("bmbbbjb", "SFDC31E", "SERINO='"+STOK60T.GetFieldValue("SERINO",SatirNo)+"'","", -1)
		
		
		if S01.NREF01(1) = 1 then
				res = pe1.Write(satirpozr , xCizgi(1)+2, 6, grid(2,1),Doc.NumFormat(S01.NREF02(1),20,2))
		else
				res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,  xCizgi(1)+2,  6, grid(2,1),(Doc.NumFormat(S01.NREF01(1),20,0)&"x"&Doc.NumFormat(S01.NREF02(1),20,2)))
		end if
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,  xCizgi(2)+2, 6, grid(3,1),Doc.NumFormat(S01.NREF03(1),20,3))
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,  xCizgi(3)+2,  6, grid(4,1),Doc.NumFormat(S01.NREF04(1),20,0))
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,  xCizgi(4)+2,  6, grid(5,1),Doc.NumFormat(S01.NREF05(1),20,6))
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,  xCizgi(5)+2, 6, grid(6,1),Doc.NumFormat(S01.NREF06(1),20,2))
		
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr , xCizgi(6)+2,  6, grid(7,1), Doc.NumFormat(Doc.Div(STOK60T.GetFieldValue("SF_MIKTAR",SatirNo),S01.NREF06(1))	,20,3))
			
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,  xCizgi(7)+2,  6, grid(8,1), Doc.NumFormat(Doc.Div(1,(Doc.Div(S01.NREF03(1)*S01.NREF03(1)*3.14*S01.NREF05(1),4))),20,2))
		'Doc.Div(1,(Doc.Div(S01.NREF03(1)*S01.NREF03(1)*3.14*S01.NREF05(1),4)))
		
		Set S02 = Doc.Select1("bmbbbjb", "STOK20T", "SERINO='"+STOK60T.GetFieldValue("SERINO",SatirNo)+"'","", -1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,  xCizgi(8)+2, 6,grid(9,1),Doc.NumFormat(S02.BRUT(1),15,1))
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,  xCizgi(10)+2,  6,grid(11,1),Doc.NumFormat(S02.DARA(1),15,1))
		TopBrut = TopBrut + S02.BRUT(1)
		TopDara = TopDara +S02.DARA(1)
		TopNet = TopNet + STOK60T.GetFieldValue("SF_MIKTAR",SatirNo)
	
		'res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol+75, satiryuksekligi, 20, Doc.NumFormat(S01.NREF02(1),20,2))
		'res = pe1.Write(Baslangic-25 , xCizgi(9)-5 , 12, 25, doc.NumFormat(toporjtutar,8,3) )
		'res = pe1.Write(Baslangic-11 , xCizgi(4), 6,grid(6,1)-2,Toplammiktar)
					 
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub




Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
	
	
'dýþ çerçeve
	res = pe1.box(8, 10, satirSonu - 3, SayfaEni - 20)

'dikey çizgiler
	res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
	For i = 0 To UBound(xCizgi) -1
		LineX = cint(xCizgi(i))
		If LineX = 0 Then Exit For
		res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 14, LineX, satirSonu - satirBaslangic + 19, 0)
	Next
End Sub




Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(12,6)
	
	Grid(0,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(1,0) = "SERINO"
	Grid(2,0) = "NREF02"
	Grid(10,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"

	Grid(0,1) = 26
	Grid(1,1) = 20
	Grid(2,1) = 18
	Grid(3,1) = 14	
	Grid(4,1) = 12
	Grid(5,1) = 17	
	Grid(6,1) = 12	
	Grid(7,1) = 14
	Grid(8,1) = 15
	Grid(9,1) = 15
	Grid(10,1) = 15
	Grid(11,1) = 12
	
	
	
	
	Grid(0,2) = "Stok Kodu"						
	Grid(1,2) = "Kalite Kontrol No"
	Grid(2,2) = "Anma Çapý"
	Grid(3,2) = "Ölçülen Çap"
	Grid(4,2) = "% Uzama"
	Grid(5,2) = "D.C. Direnci"
	Grid(6,2) = "Birim Aðýrlýk"
	Grid(7,2) = "Metraj"
	Grid(8,2) = "Ýletkenlik"
	Grid(9,2) = "Brüt Kg"
	Grid(10,2) = "Net Kg"
	Grid(11,2) = "Dara Kg"
	
	
End Sub




Sub Normal(pe1)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	res = pe1.setitalic(0)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
	res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
	res = pe1.setrotation(0)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.setalign("left")
End Sub
Function FontAyarla(BasilacakAlan,ToplamGen,Font)
	Oran = BasilacakAlan / ToplamGen
	If Oran >= 1 Then
		FontAyarla = Font
	Else
		If oran <=0.8 Then
			FontAyarla = Round(0.8 * Font,0)
		Else
			FontAyarla = Round(Oran * Font,0)
		End If
	End If
End Function                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               


----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : HAK6_2014 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : F-FORM
Hangi Tablo için : PFRM7S
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : 
Kod : 
Public strfind(8)
Sub PrintForm()
	
	Dim objDoc, objWord, Selection
	Set objWord = CreateObject( "Word.Application" )
	objWord.visible = True
	Set objDoc = objWord.Documents.Add()
	Set Selection = objWord.Selection
	'objWord.Selection.EndKey 6,0
	FirmKodu = Trim(Doc.Getd7FirmCode())
	Uygulama = Trim(Doc.GetDocType)

	Set PFR7SE = Doc.Select1("dd333d", "PFRM7SE", "", "EVRAKNO", -1)
	'Set MKR7SE = Doc.Select1("dd333d", "MAKR7SE", "EVRAKNO = '_GNPRG1'", "", -1)
	
	KayitSayisi = PFR7SE.GetRecCount()
	'-------------------------------------------------
	'Satýr boþluk ayarýný yapar.
	Selection.ParagraphFormat.LineSpacing = 10
	objWord.ActiveDocument.ShowSpellingErrors = False
	'----------------------------------------------
	'Tab ayarýný yapar.
	objWord.ActiveDocument.DefaultTabStop = 15
	'---------------------------------------------
	For i = 1 To KayitSayisi
		EVRAKKODU_ORJ = Trim(PFR7SE.EVRAKNO(i))
		If Left(EVRAKKODU_ORJ, 2) <> "++" Then
			EVRAKKODU = Trim(PFR7SE.EVRAKNO(i))

			Kodlar = ""
			EKodlar = ""
			Selection.Paragraphs.Alignment = 1   '1:wdAlignParagraphCenter yani üst satýrdakini ortalar.
			Selection.TypeText "Form Kaynak Kodu"
			Selection.Typeparagraph
		
			Selection.Paragraphs.Alignment = 3
			Selection.TypeText "Firma Kodu : " & FirmKodu  & " / " & "Uygulama : " &  Uygulama
			Selection.Typeparagraph
			Selection.TypeText "Makro Kodu : " & EVRAKKODU 
			'------------------------
		
			'Selection.Paragraphs.Alignment = 1 wdAlignParagraphCenter yani üst satýrdakini ortalar.
			'2: wdAlignParagraphRight yani üst satýrdakini saga yaslar.
			'3: wdAlignParagraphRight yani üst satýrdakini sola yaslar.
		
			Selection.Paragraphs.Alignment = 3
			Selection.Typeparagraph
			Selection.TypeText "Hangi Tablo için : " & Trim(PFR7SE.DOCTYPE(i))
			Selection.Typeparagraph
			Selection.TypeText "Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : " & Trim(PFR7SE.ADDDOCTYPES(i))
			Selection.Typeparagraph
			Selection.TypeText "Açýklama : " & Trim(PFR7SE.ACIKLAMA(i))
			Selection.Typeparagraph
			Selection.TypeText "Kod : "
			Selection.Typeparagraph

			Set PFR7ST = Doc.Select1("dd333d", "PFRM7ST", "EVRAKNO='"&EVRAKKODU&"'",">SRNUM", -1)

			For k = 1 To PFR7ST.GetRecCount()
				Selection.Font.Color = vbBlue
				Kodlar = Kodlar & PFR7ST.SCRIPTTEXT_RO(k)
			Next

			Selection.TypeText Kodlar
			Selection.Typeparagraph
			Selection.Typeparagraph
			'-----------------------------------
			Selection.Typeparagraph
			'-----------------------------------
			Selection.Font.Color = vbBlack
			Set PFR7SE= Doc.Select1("dd333d", "PFRM7SE", "", "", -1)
		
			Selection.TypeText "----------------------------------------------------------------"
			Selection.Typeparagraph
		End If
	Next
	'--------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'Font lara makyaj
	For j = 1  To 8
		strfind(1) = "Makro Kaynak Kodu"
		strfind(2) = "Makro Kodu : "
		strfind(3) = "Hangi Tablo için : "
		strfind(4) = "Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : "
		strfind(5) = "Açýklama : "
		strfind(6) = "Kod : "
		strfind(7) = "Firma Kodu : "
		strfind(8) = "Uygulama : "

		Set objRange = objWord.ActiveDocument.Content
		
		With objRange.Find
		.Text = strfind(j)
		Do
			.Execute
			If .Found Then
				objRange.Font.Color = vbRed
				objRange.Font.Bold = True
				'If j = 1 Then objWord.ParagraphFormat.Alignment = wdAlignParagraphCenter
			End If
			Loop While .Found
			End With
		Next
		Selection.WholeStory
		'----------------------------------------------------
		'Satýr sonlarýndaki boþluðu alma
		Selection.ParagraphFormat.SpaceAfter = 0.5
		'---------------------------------------------------
		'Sayfanýn kenarlarýný daralt
		With objWord.ActiveDocument.PageSetup
			.Orientation = wdOrientLandscape
			bosluk = 15
			.TopMargin = bosluk
			.BottomMargin = bosluk
			.LeftMargin = 43
			.RightMargin = bosluk
		End With
		'--------------------------------------------------------------------------
		'Satýr numaralarý ekler
    With Selection.PageSetup
        With .LineNumbering
            .Active = True
            .StartingNumber = 1
            .CountBy = 1
            .RestartMode = 1
            .DistanceFromText = 0
        End With
		End With
		'--------------------------------------------------------------------------
		objWord.visible = True
		desktoppath = CreateObject("WScript.Shell").SpecialFolders("Desktop")
		objDoc.SaveAs(desktoppath & "\" & FirmKodu & "_" & Uygulama)
		Msgbox "Masaüstüne kaydedildi..."
		Set objDoc = Nothing
		Set oWords = Nothing
		Set objWord = Nothing
End Sub
